Committee of Appropriations.

**[00:13:02.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000242)  
Also to our sponsor,

**[00:13:04.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000243)  
Representative Albert

**[00:13:06.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000244)  
Garcia. I will

**[00:13:08.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000245)  
present to you the

**[00:13:10.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000246)  
National Expenditure

**[00:13:12.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000247)  
Program for Fiscal Year 2027

**[00:13:14.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000248)  
for the Department of Health.

**[00:13:16.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000249)  
Can we put up the

**[00:13:19.890] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000250)  
first slide, please? Bakit wala? Bakit hindi pa?

**[00:14:32.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000251)  
We sent po and checked the

**[00:14:34.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000252)  
copy earlier. It's with the

**[00:14:36.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000253)  
committee na po.

**[00:14:50.930] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000254)  
May we request our technical

**[00:14:52.930] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000255)  
team to

**[00:14:54.930] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000256)  
display the presentation

**[00:14:56.930] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000257)  
of DOH on the screen?

**[00:16:33.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000258)  
So, while waiting for the presentation of the DOH, may we first acknowledge the chairman of the Committee on Health.

**[00:16:45.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000259)  
May we recognize Chair Gato for his manifestation habang iniintay po natin yung presentation ng DOH.

**[00:16:52.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000260)  
Chair Gato, please proceed.

**[00:16:54.320] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000261)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, esteemed colleagues, Secretary Edwin Mercado and family, DOH family. Magandang umaga po.

**[00:17:07.440] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000262)  
Sabi nila, walang kinikilalang yaman o estado sa buhay ang sakit. Illnesses do not discriminate.

**[00:17:16.000] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000263)  
Yes, they do. Because while a disease like cancer may afflict anyone, it does not affect everyone equally.

**[00:17:28.280] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000264)  
The difference is often not in the disease itself, but in what comes after the diagnosis.

**[00:17:35.400] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000265)  
Who can begin treatment immediately, who has to wait, who can sustain years of care, and who is forced into debt simply to keep going.

**[00:17:47.580] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000266)  
And sometimes, the difference is simply who gets to survive.

**[00:17:52.420] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000267)  
This is the reality that the Department of Health

**[00:17:55.060] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000268)  
continues to confront, while carrying the enormous responsibility of protecting the health and well-being of more than 100 million Filipinos.

**[00:18:07.100] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000269)  
The challenges before the DOH are immense and constantly evolving.

**[00:18:12.060] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000270)  
Yet, it continues to strengthen primary and public health care, expand access to essential medicines and life-saving interventions,

**[00:18:20.880] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000271)  
advance universal health care, strengthen local health services,

**[00:18:25.060] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000272)  
support our healthcare workers, and protect our communities through prevention and health promotion, like immunization.

**[00:18:34.620] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000273)  
Mr. Chair, as Chair of the Department of Health, as Chair of the Committee on Health, the House of Representatives,

**[00:18:43.440] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000274)  
may I express my full and unequivocal support for the proposed budget of the Department of Health.

**[00:18:49.500] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000275)  
Bung puso po natin sinusuportahan ang panukalang budget ng Department of Health.

**[00:18:55.320] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000276)  
Because health is a right and not a privilege, at ang karapatan nito ay hindi dapat nakadepende sa kakayahan magbayad.

**[00:19:04.440] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000277)  
No Filipino should have to delay treatment because they cannot afford it.

**[00:19:08.980] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000278)  
No family should have to choose between continuing a course of treatment and meeting their most basic needs.

**[00:19:16.580] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000279)  
And no one should be left behind simply because the care they need is beyond their means.

**[00:19:25.060] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000281)  
Our budget must reflect this principle. Mr. Chair, my dear colleagues, let us give the DOH the capacity to respond, our healthcare workers the support they deserve, and our communities the services they need.

**[00:19:41.120] Rep. Ciriaco B. Gato Jr., M.D., FPSO-HNS** (asr-000282)  
Let us approve a health budget that is not merely adequate, but responsive to the realities of our people, equitable in its reach, and worthy of the right to health that we have pledged to uphold. Maraming salamat, Mr. Chair. Mabuhay ang healthcare workers. Mabuhay ang Department of Health. Thank you.

**[00:20:07.370] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000293)  
Thank you very much, Chair Siriaco Gato, Committee on Health, for your manifestation of support to the budget of the Department of Health.

**[00:20:14.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000294)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:20:16.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000295)  
So, I see that the presentation is now flashed on the screen.

**[00:20:21.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000296)  
So, I would like to recognize once again.

**[00:20:24.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000297)  
So, once again, our Secretary of Health, Dr. Edwin Mercado, for your presentation.

**[00:20:30.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000298)  
Please proceed.

**[00:20:31.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000299)  
Thank you.

**[00:20:31.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000300)  
Maraming salamat po, Rep. Kong Abet Garcia.

**[00:20:35.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000301)  
At muli po, magandang umaga po sa lahat po ng ating members ng Appropriations Committee.

**[00:20:42.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000302)  
Ito po yung ating presentation on the 2027 National Healthcare Expenditure.

**[00:20:48.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000303)  
Next slide po.

**[00:20:51.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000304)  
So, ang ating pong vision is to make the Filipinos among the best.

**[00:20:54.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000305)  
The healthiest people in Asia by 2014.

**[00:20:57.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000306)  
So, that means also making sure that they have access to quality healthcare.

**[00:21:01.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000307)  
And as Kong Gato mentioned earlier, financial risk protection.

**[00:21:05.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000308)  
So, ang ating pong hangad ay talagang yung pagpapalatag po ng ating universal healthcare program, which I will expound on later.

**[00:21:18.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000309)  
Next slide po.

**[00:21:19.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000310)  
So, nung ako po ay natalaga bilang kaliin po ng kalusugan.

**[00:21:24.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000311)  
Three weeks ago, nag-draft na po kami ng Unified Health Agenda,

**[00:21:29.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000312)  
ang katawagin po namin ay UNA, with PCO Bev Ho.

**[00:21:34.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000313)  
For the first time po, nagkaroon ng common agenda po ang DOH at PhilHealth.

**[00:21:39.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000314)  
At pinagkabit po namin yung mga programa ng PhilHealth na dapat pong bigyan din ng suporta ng DOH and vice versa po.

**[00:21:47.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000315)  
Mga programa rin ng DOH na kailangan pong suportahan ng ating PhilHealth.

**[00:21:54.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000316)  
So, ang...

**[00:21:54.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000317)  
Ang stronger alignment is along four or five major pillars.

**[00:22:00.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000318)  
Ang una po dyan ay yung healthy schools, workplace, and communities, which will be focusing on prevention, health promotion, early screening, and linking to PhilHealth benefits like the YACAP and the gamot.

**[00:22:12.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000319)  
So, i-embed na rin po namin dyan yung ating pong mga health programs para po at the level of the settings na kung saan po natin imimit mismo yung ating mga kababayan,

**[00:22:22.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000320)  
ay mabibigyan na po natin.

**[00:22:24.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000321)  
Ang all-in-one po na approach itong ating primary health care.

**[00:22:29.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000322)  
So, ang pangalawa nga po, patungo dun sa primary health care, ay tiyakin din pong merong access sa mga doktor, sa mga gamot, at sa mga procedures na at least essential procedures po na i-cover natin.

**[00:22:43.180] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000323)  
Kaya, gaya po, at didiscuss naman po ito ng PCO Bev, palalawakin po yung coverage ng ating YACAP sa kasalukuyang labing dalawa, labing tatlong lab test tomorrow.

**[00:22:54.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000324)  
And also the gamot.

**[00:22:57.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000325)  
Yung pangatatlo po ay equality and efficient hospital.

**[00:23:00.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000326)  
So, ako po ay mag-create ng hospital operations cluster.

**[00:23:05.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000327)  
Kasi almost 40% po ng budget ng DOH are on hospital operations.

**[00:23:10.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000328)  
So, yun po yung pinakamalaking cost component ng aming budget.

**[00:23:15.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000329)  
Kaya, kailangan din po natin siguruduhin na efficient yung pagde-deliver ng ating mga hospital.

**[00:23:21.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000330)  
At yun naman po yung aking, kung tawagin, 40.

**[00:23:24.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000331)  
Kasi ito po ang aking ginawa nung ako po ay nasa private sector pa lang.

**[00:23:29.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000332)  
Ang pang-apat po ay yung information technology transformation.

**[00:23:33.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000333)  
So easier access and front-facing of our patients.

**[00:23:37.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000334)  
Kung ano po, ito po rin yung ating pagkukuna ng datos para po yung ating mga health programs ay mas maging data-driven evidence-based.

**[00:23:45.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000335)  
At ang pang-lima po ay strong institution and health workforce, particularly to address the persistent health workforce issues.

**[00:23:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000336)  
And service delivery gaps.

**[00:23:56.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000337)  
So the end goal po is to move UHC from policy to practice and to ensure that its benefits are actually experienced by every Filipino.

**[00:24:04.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000338)  
So we will be more proactive in reaching out to our, at hindi po yung hinihintay pang sila ay magkasakit para pumasok dito sa ating healthcare system.

**[00:24:14.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000339)  
Next slide please.

**[00:24:15.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000340)  
So gaya po ng nabanggit ni Kong Abet, ang kabuan po ng aming budget ay 353.8 billion.

**[00:24:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000341)  
Based po on our approved NEP.

**[00:24:26.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000342)  
At along our five pillars, dinistribute po namin itong ating budget.

**[00:24:31.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000343)  
At ito po yung pakikita ko mamaya kung bakit po ganito ang aming distribution at ano po yung mga programang ating gagawin.

**[00:24:39.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000344)  
So next slide please.

**[00:24:42.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000345)  
So sa kabuan po in the current administration of President Ferdinand Marcos Jr.

**[00:24:49.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000346)  
Ay 1.97 trillion na po ang talagang nailaan.

**[00:24:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000347)  
Para po sa healthcare.

**[00:24:54.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000348)  
So ito po ay significant dahil sa unang pagkakataon ho, yung ating healthcare expenditure or current healthcare expenditure noong 2025.

**[00:25:04.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000349)  
Base po doon sa ating Philippine Statistics Authority.

**[00:25:08.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000350)  
Yun po yung unang pagkakataon na yung ating government spending ay humigit kaysa doon sa out-of-pocket spending.

**[00:25:16.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000351)  
Ang may isa lang pong one-off noong COVID.

**[00:25:19.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000352)  
Kasi marami talaga pong naglagay tayo, naglaan.

**[00:25:23.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000353)  
Marami.

**[00:25:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000354)  
Ang ating government spending ay humigit kaysa doon sa out-of-pocket spending.

**[00:25:25.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000355)  
Pero ngayon po on regular basis, ang healthcare expenditure of government increased from 42.1% in 2023 to current 46.5% in 2025.

**[00:25:37.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000356)  
Such that the out-of-pocket spending of the Filipinos were brought down from 44.4% to 41.2%.

**[00:25:45.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000357)  
So ito'y pagpapatunay pong kumakapit na po yung mga programa natin.

**[00:25:50.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000358)  
So ito pong ating 2027.

**[00:25:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000359)  
And NEP, ay gaya ng nabanggit ni Kong Abet, ay 4.9, ay mas mababa po ng 21.2% compared to 2026 GAA.

**[00:26:05.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000360)  
Bagamat ito po ay 10% higher noong 2026 NEP.

**[00:26:09.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000361)  
Pero sa kabuan po, ito ay nagkoconstitute po ng 4.9% of our 7.2 trillion national budget and 1.1% of the projected 2027 GDP.

**[00:26:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000362)  
Kung tutusin po, medyo mababa pa rin po kasi ang usual po na GDP health to GDP ratio po is nasa 8 to 10%.

**[00:26:32.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000363)  
So tayo po ay, although ito po ay spending lang ng government, if you say that government is spending 46%, assuming 1%,

**[00:26:40.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000364)  
kung inidagdag po natin yung private spending, abot pa rin po ng mga 2.53%.

**[00:26:46.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000365)  
Medyo mababa po compared dun sa talaga pong dapat na ibigay for healthcare.

**[00:26:51.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000366)  
But health remains a majority.

**[00:26:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000367)  
National priority and investment in terms of human capital.

**[00:26:56.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000368)  
Next slide po.

**[00:26:58.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000369)  
So gaya ng nasabi ko po, ang pinakamalaking component po nitong ating budget ay sa maintenance and other operating expense.

**[00:27:06.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000370)  
Nakasama naman po dito sa MOOE, yung iba pong mga subscription for managed services, etc.

**[00:27:14.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000371)  
Kaya nga po gaya ng nasabi ko, bulk of this will be coming from hospital operations.

**[00:27:19.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000372)  
That's why I want to really make sure that this is properly used.

**[00:27:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000373)  
Ang personal service po ay tumaas ng 77% to 117 billion.

**[00:27:29.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000374)  
Ito po ay dahil dun sa full implementation na po ng ating salary standardization law.

**[00:27:35.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000375)  
Nasa huling yugtunan po tayo dun sa implementation ng SSL.

**[00:27:39.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000376)  
Yung capital outlay po yung medyo malaki po yung nabawas, kumpara nun 2026.

**[00:27:45.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000377)  
Ito po ay bumaba ng 35%.

**[00:27:47.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000378)  
At karamihan pa po dito sa 19, or hindi naman karamihan, may portion po dito sa 19 billion.

**[00:27:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000379)  
Almost 4.2 billion po ay nakalaan na po para dun sa Health System Resilience Project

**[00:27:59.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000380)  
na tayo po ay merong World Bank loan na na-approve po nung 2025.

**[00:28:03.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000381)  
At yun nga po, pinupush na naming magamit.

**[00:28:07.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000382)  
Ito po ay nakalaan sa 17 probinsya po, ayon po dun sa ating naging loan agreement with the World Bank.

**[00:28:15.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000383)  
Yung iba naman po ay HVEP, 14.5 billion, which includes the facilities, infrastructures, and the equipment.

**[00:28:22.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000384)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:28:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000385)  
So nais ko lang po ngayong himayin yung mga programa po natin kada pilar.

**[00:28:30.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000386)  
So ito po ang unang pilar na Healthy Schools, Workplace, and Communities.

**[00:28:35.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000387)  
Karamihan po dyan ay yung mga syntax revenue for the payment of the indirect contributors of PhilHealth.

**[00:28:43.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000388)  
So ito po ay sumasaklaw sa 19.3 indirect contributors, 55.4.

**[00:28:49.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000389)  
Pero kung tutuusin po natin yung gastusin ng PhilHealth,

**[00:28:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000390)  
para po i-cover yung benefit expense nitong ating mga indirect members, medyo po kulang po yung 55.4 million.

**[00:29:02.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000391)  
Sa kasulukuyan po, ipapaliwanag naman ni PCEO Bevian, pero sumisipa na po yung indirect members

**[00:29:10.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000392)  
kasi meron na po silang consciousness na sila pala ay merong beneficio at immediate eligibility.

**[00:29:16.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000393)  
So nakikita po talaga namin nag-avail na.

**[00:29:19.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000394)  
Yung 2.5 billion po ay for health promotion.

**[00:29:23.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000395)  
Kakabit na natin dun sa setting-based impanelment ng YACAP.

**[00:29:27.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000396)  
Itong kasabay na yung health promotion, lalong-lalo na po dun sa ating mga schools

**[00:29:33.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000397)  
para po maturuan na kaagad natin yung ating mga kabataan, ano po yung tamang mga habits to be healthy.

**[00:29:39.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000398)  
At yung 1.3 billion po ay sa health emergency preparedness, response, quarantine service, and international health surveillance.

**[00:29:46.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000399)  
Di po lingid sa atin, meron po tayong problema ngayon ng HACE dahil po cross-boundary na po ang issue.

**[00:29:53.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000400)  
Pati po yung mga emerging diseases like Ebola, yun po yung transfer.

**[00:30:00.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000401)  
na po o zoonotic disease na nagmumula sa mga hayop, lumilipat na po sa mga tao.

**[00:30:05.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000402)  
Ito po ay sanhinan ng ating global warming, kaya po talagang dapat paglaanan at pagpagpreparahan ito po ang ating response na ito.

**[00:30:16.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000403)  
Next po.

**[00:30:19.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000404)  
So ito po ang bunga ng ating focus sa ating primary care system.

**[00:30:23.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000405)  
Two out of three children are fully immunized, although ito po ay mababa pa dun sa ating target na 95%.

**[00:30:30.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000406)  
So ito po talaga ipupush natin.

**[00:30:33.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000407)  
Yung eight out of ten children aged two years old and below with adequate height and age, ito po yung stunting,

**[00:30:40.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000408)  
ay medyo bumaba po from 22.5% ng 2023 to 21.1% for 2025.

**[00:30:49.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000409)  
Bagumat yun po ay malayo pa rin po dun sa ating mga target.

**[00:30:52.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000410)  
Lalo na po dun sa millennium development goal targets natin.

**[00:30:57.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000411)  
Yung pong household na may access sa basic water supply ay nasa 82.74%.

**[00:31:03.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000412)  
Ang target po natin ay 94%.

**[00:31:05.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000413)  
Yung pong mga Pilipinong pamilya na meron pong access sa modern family planning methods ay nasa 64.5%.

**[00:31:14.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000414)  
Ang target po natin ay 72.5%.

**[00:31:17.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000415)  
At ang may TB po, meron naman po tayong 85%.

**[00:31:22.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000416)  
Na yun po ang ating target na accomplished po natin na yung pong nadadiagnose ng TB ay nabibigyan ng gamot.

**[00:31:29.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000417)  
At ito pong ating Pilipino na living with HIV, medyo malayo pa rin dun sa ating target na 95% kasi ngayon po ay nasa 66% pa rin po.

**[00:31:40.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000418)  
Pero meron na po kaming ginagawang mga programa na palialawigin po yung OHAT program ng PhilHealth

**[00:31:47.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000419)  
na iko-cover na rin yung ating mga preparatory or preemptive.

**[00:31:52.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000420)  
At yung testing po palalawigin din natin para po maitaas natin dun sa ating target na 95%.

**[00:32:01.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000421)  
At dun naman po sa mental health ay 262,000 po ang ating individual na nakakuha po ng gamot for mental health.

**[00:32:10.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000422)  
Next slide please.

**[00:32:13.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000423)  
Kasama po din dito sa ating first, second pillar na functional primary care,

**[00:32:18.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000424)  
yung ating pong family health, immunization, nutrition, and responsible care.

**[00:32:22.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000425)  
At yung ating parenting, which is pegged at 9.1 billion.

**[00:32:28.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000426)  
At ito po ay tatargetin po natin yung mga vulnerable population.

**[00:32:32.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000427)  
Gaya po ng mga senior citizen, mga may edad na, mga bata, at yung po meron ng pre-existing disease.

**[00:32:39.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000428)  
Ganon din po sa communicable disease prevention, which includes TB and HIV, 6.9 billion.

**[00:32:46.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000429)  
Sa non-communicable disease, ito naman po yung mga emerging diseases due to modern living,

**[00:32:52.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000430)  
depression, diabetes, mental health, 2.7 billion.

**[00:32:56.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000431)  
At dito po sa local health system development, dahil gusto nga natin, because of our devolved nature,

**[00:33:02.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000432)  
talaga pong matulungan yung local government units na palakasin yung kanilang local health system,

**[00:33:07.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000433)  
ay may nakalaan po tayong 1.5 billion, which will cover 110 UHC integration sites.

**[00:33:14.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000434)  
Next slide po.

**[00:33:17.150] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000435)  
Kasama pa rin po dito sa ating next pillar ay yung ating pong mga functional primary care,

**[00:33:24.470] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000436)  
system, nakikita po dito na ang ating pong benefit payment noong 2025 sa PhilHealth ay umabot po ng higit 300 billion.

**[00:33:34.410] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000437)  
Ito po ay 82% increase from the previous year, reflecting the significant expansion of PhilHealth financing for member healthcare access.

**[00:33:43.570] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000438)  
Kasama rin po dito yung pagpapalabak po ng yakap na sa kasalukuyan po mula 27.8 million noong 2024,

**[00:33:52.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000439)  
ay umabot po ng 40 million.

**[00:33:54.370] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000440)  
na Pilipinos ang registered with YAKAP. Although may mga ginawa po kaming programa para linisin yung tala ng YAKAP, kasi may mga old YAKAP na automatic empanelment na nagre-reklamo po sa amin bakit kami na-empanel, di naman namin

**[00:34:08.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000441)  
alam. So tinanggal po namin yun

**[00:34:10.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000442)  
at naglilinis po kami. Kasi meron na po moving forward ng biometrics

**[00:34:14.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000443)  
para matiyak na yung pinagkakakilanlan ng ating pasyente ay talagang

**[00:34:18.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000444)  
siya 'yun bago ma-enroll

**[00:34:20.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000445)  
dito sa YAKAP. Meron po tayong

**[00:34:22.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000446)  
40,000 cancer patients

**[00:34:24.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000447)  
na nakatanggap sa Cancer Assistance Fund.

**[00:34:27.370] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000450)  
Meron po tayong 2,000 health infrastructure projects na na-kompleto mula 2023 hanggang 2025.

**[00:34:33.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000451)  
Meron po tayong higit limang, almost 5,000 medical equipment procured and delivered

**[00:34:39.070] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000452)  
and nearly 500 medical transport vehicles provided for emergency response and patient referral.

**[00:34:45.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000453)  
Actually, ito pong ambulansya, meron na rin po tayong coverage of Hill Health.

**[00:34:50.370] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000454)  
Pati ito po yung mga registered or licensed ambulance.

**[00:34:53.330] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000455)  
Pinag-aaralan po kung pwede na rin maka-cover yung patient transport vehicles

**[00:34:57.590] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000456)  
dahil yun naman po yung pinakamalimit na nagagamit sa pagtatransfer po ng ating pasyente.

**[00:35:02.990] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000457)  
Next slide po.

**[00:35:04.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000458)  
So dito naman po sa ating mga hospital, gaya na nasabi ko po sa inyo kanina,

**[00:35:10.470] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000459)  
102.9 billion of the budget is going to the 71 DOH regional hospital

**[00:35:17.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000460)  
which total has about 20,000 licensed bed

**[00:35:21.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000461)  
and also 26.9 billion for the operation of the 12 DOH hospitals in Metro Manila

**[00:35:28.310] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000462)  
with 9,000 licensed bed

**[00:35:30.410] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000463)  
at ang 8.1 billion naman po ay for the government-owned and specialty hospital

**[00:35:35.510] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000464)  
kasama po dyan ang Lung Center, NKTI, ang Philippine Heart Center

**[00:35:40.530] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000465)  
at saka po ang Philippine Children's Medical Center.

**[00:35:42.830] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000466)  
Kung tutuusin po ang total number of beds po nitong tatlong classification or buckets ng ating DOH hospital,

**[00:35:51.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000467)  
ito ng 28% of the total bed capacity of 118,000 for the whole country.

**[00:35:57.010] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000468)  
So malaking portion po talaga ay provided by DOH hospitals.

**[00:36:02.830] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000469)  
Karagdagan pong 19 billion doon sa PhilHealth benefit package improvement

**[00:36:07.070] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000470)  
kasi nagkaroon na po tayo ng roadmap kung ano po yung mga panibagong mga benepisyong ilalabas sa susunod pong taon.

**[00:36:15.310] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000471)  
Karamihan po dyan pagpapalawig ng cancer,

**[00:36:18.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000472)  
pagkasama po yung iba pa nating mga sakit,

**[00:36:21.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000473)  
at ito naman pong HVEP ay nabanggit ko na kanina,

**[00:36:25.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000474)  
514 health facilities, 845 equipment project, and 345 medical transport vehicle.

**[00:36:32.710] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000475)  
Next po.

**[00:36:34.450] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000476)  
So ang pang-apat na pillar po ay itong iting ITE transformation.

**[00:36:39.750] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000477)  
So nakita ko rin po na doon sa health resilience loan natin sa World Bank ay meron pong component ng IT.

**[00:36:47.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000478)  
So yun po yung itatap ko para na maumpisa na po natin.

**[00:36:51.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000479)  
Yung very basic NHDR o yung pong interoperability layer ng ating kahit po yung primary care muna,

**[00:36:59.270] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000480)  
maikapit na natin dahil po nire-require na namin yung mga yakap facilities na kumuha po ng compliant na EMR provider

**[00:37:08.050] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000481)  
para po mas madali na nating maikonect.

**[00:37:11.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000482)  
Kasi po ang magiging resulta nito,

**[00:37:14.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000483)  
pag nagawa na po for example yung isang x-ray o isang lab test sa isang clinic,

**[00:37:19.090] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000484)  
hindi na kailangan ulitin.

**[00:37:20.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000485)  
Kapag ang pasyente po ay lumipat ng panibagong facility.

**[00:37:24.530] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000486)  
Kasi ngayon po, inuulit eh.

**[00:37:26.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000487)  
So medyo may tapon po tayo doon.

**[00:37:29.570] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000488)  
Kaya kung meron na po tayong integration, mas mababawasan po yun.

**[00:37:33.650] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000489)  
Kasama rin po yung epidemiology and surveillance dito,

**[00:37:36.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000490)  
saka yung health statistics na gaya ng sinabi ko,

**[00:37:38.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000491)  
itatap din namin dito sa NHDR para makuha po natin yung live data.

**[00:37:44.050] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000492)  
Kasi ngayon po, ang datos kinukuha lang upon reporting of the LGU health units,

**[00:37:50.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000493)  
minsan po may lag time eh.

**[00:37:52.370] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000494)  
Whereas kapag ito ay through the EMR,

**[00:37:54.770] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000495)  
mas madali po nating makukuha live yung ating data.

**[00:38:01.010] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000496)  
Ang huli po nating pillar ay yung strong institution and health workforce.

**[00:38:06.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000497)  
So tuloy pa rin po yung ating national health workforce support system.

**[00:38:11.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000498)  
Meron po tayong 24,381 health professionals that are deployed nationwide.

**[00:38:17.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000499)  
At ito po yung tumutulong lalo na po doon sa ating mga

**[00:38:20.770] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000500)  
third, fourth, fifth class municipalities.

**[00:38:23.330] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000501)  
At support din po ng ating mga karagdagang district hospitals for specialists and also for medical care.

**[00:38:30.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000502)  
At meron din po tayong 1.2 billion for supply chain management.

**[00:38:35.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000503)  
So ang tinitingnan nga po namin ay mas paigtingin pa itong ating IT system ng supply chain

**[00:38:41.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000504)  
para talaga po makuha natin yung mga balances down to the level of the CHD.

**[00:38:47.090] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000505)  
At ma-predict po natin yung ating turnaround time ng delivery ng ating mga gamot.

**[00:38:52.370] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000510)  
So ito po ay ang kabuan po. Next slide po.

**[00:38:56.770] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000511)  
Tungo po sa isang health sector na una sa pagtutulungan, una sa pag-usad, una sa pagkakilala.

**[00:39:04.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000512)  
Ito po yung ating internal directive at ito po ang internal messaging natin sa ating mga DOH and DOH family.

**[00:39:13.410] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000513)  
Upang maramdaman po ng ating mga kababayan ang isang servisyong pangkalusugan na mabilis, patas at mapagkakatiwalaan.

**[00:39:19.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000515)  
pagkakatiwalaan. Thank you very much

**[00:39:22.290] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000516)  
po.

**[00:43:51.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000649)

**[00:43:57.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000652)

**[00:43:59.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000653)

**[00:44:01.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000654)

**[00:44:03.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000655)

**[00:44:19.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000663)

**[00:44:21.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000664)

**[00:44:25.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000666)

**[00:44:27.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000667)

**[00:44:31.200] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000669)  
Yun muna po, Mr. Chair.

**[00:44:42.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000673)

**[00:44:44.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000674)  
Currently po,

**[00:44:46.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000675)  
meron pong

**[00:44:48.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000676)  
approved board resolution

**[00:44:50.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000677)  
po regarding differential

**[00:44:52.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000678)  
benefits. Pero

**[00:44:54.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000679)  
it's not yet

**[00:44:56.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000680)  
still being implemented kasi po may

**[00:44:58.560] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000681)  
pinag-aaralan pa po kami.

**[00:44:59.820] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000682)  
na mga implications niya in terms of, say, will it increase medical inflation, for example,

**[00:45:08.180] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000683)  
at in terms of our updating of our database, how accurate is it for us to be able to assert

**[00:45:17.160] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000684)  
na itong member po to consistent po siyang nakabaya during the last 18 months.

**[00:45:23.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000685)  
Claro po sa amin na may efforts po kami kailangan gawin in terms of better collection efficiency

**[00:45:30.580] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000686)  
kasi po among the direct contributors, marami pong nagbabayad na members

**[00:45:35.860] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000687)  
but at the same time, marami rin po na hindi sakto at consistent yung binabayad po nila.

**[00:45:42.760] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000688)  
So, of course, initially the policy directive of the board was to encourage direct contributors

**[00:45:52.000] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000689)  
to pay more consistently.

**[00:45:53.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000690)  
Na tuloy-tuloy.

**[00:45:55.280] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000691)  
But we also are aware of possible implications of putting forward the policy.

**[00:46:05.140] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000692)  
So, Deputy Minority Leader, at this point, we're studying all the implications of implementing the policy.

**[00:46:15.960] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000693)  
So, may board directive kami pero we're just completing all of our operationalities

**[00:46:23.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000694)  
and organization processes.

**[00:46:25.100] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000695)  
Kung tayo po ang tatanungin, Mr. Chair, sana huwag nyo na pong i-implement ito.

**[00:46:30.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000696)  
Alam naman po natin na when we speak of universal healthcare, malinaw yung mandato.

**[00:46:36.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000697)  
Gobyerno dapat ang nag-cover ng premium ng mga indirect contributors.

**[00:46:40.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000698)  
Tanong po, nakukuha po ba nang buo ng PhilHealth ang earmark amount na dapat tinatanggap nito from SYNTAXIS?

**[00:46:47.670] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000699)
