**[00:46:57.420] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000700)  
Yes po. Meron po kaming list po nung projected SYNTAXIS revenues and how much we're getting annually.

**[00:47:07.420] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000701)  
Sir Rene.

**[00:47:09.420] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000702)  
Mr. Chair, for PhilHealth po, we got 69.78 billion pesos GAA for this year.

**[00:47:26.120] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000703)  
And sa net po ng 2027, we have 74 billion po, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:32.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000704)  
Magkano naman po ito po? Go ahead po.

**[00:47:37.490] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000705)  
Ang SYNTAXIS po dito ay nasa 53 billion po ngayong taon.

**[00:47:42.490] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000706)  
And for the next year po ay nasa 54 billion pesos, Mr. Chair.

**[00:47:48.490] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000707)  
Magkano naman po at ako kuha mula sa PCSO at sa PAGCOR?

**[00:47:54.120] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000708)  
Ang PCSO and PAGCOR po, Mr. Chair, mayroon po tayong nasa GAA ng 2023 and 2024 of 21.17 billion pesos.

**[00:48:07.710] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000709)  
Mayroon po tayong nasa PCSO at sa PAGCOR of 21.17 billion every year.

**[00:48:11.710] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000710)  
Based on our calculation, Mr. Chair, we have as of second quarter ng 2026, we have 127.558 billion.

**[00:48:21.710] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000711)  
Pero ito pa lang po ang naiintala doon sa ating GAA.

**[00:48:25.710] Renato L. Limsiaco Jr.** (asr-000712)  
So meaning, we still have 85.2 billion pesos that are not yet reflected in our GAA, Mr. Chair.

**[00:48:34.710] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000713)  
Still on PhilHealth po.

**[00:48:38.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000714)  
Kaya ako po tinatanong ito dahil gusto po nating maintindihan ng publiko na mayroon po nakukuhang pondo ang PhilHealth

**[00:48:46.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000715)  
para po makatulong makover yung pangangailangan po ng ating mga indirect contributors at hindi po sila kailangan itrato bilang second class citizens.

**[00:48:53.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000716)  
Punta naman po tayo doon sa YACAP.

**[00:48:55.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000717)  
Sa ngayon po, alam po ba natin kung ilan na po ang LGU na may YACAP accredited facilities?

**[00:49:01.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000718)  
This could be an actual number or a percentage.

**[00:49:03.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000719)  
At ilan po sa kanila nakaka-comply sa requirements ng PhilHealth

**[00:49:07.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000720)  
para maging YACAP provider?

**[00:49:09.370] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000721)  
Ang alam po namin, based sa records po namin, 92% po ng ating LGU may at least one YACAP provider po.

**[00:49:19.550] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000722)  
Salamat po. Thank you po sa PhilHealth.

**[00:49:24.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000723)  
Inulit ko lang po yung punto about kung ang bawat buhay mahalaga, dapat walang first class at second class citizen.

**[00:49:31.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000724)  
Ikaw man ay direct or indirect contributor.

**[00:49:34.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000725)  
Ang mga indirect contributors po hindi sila pabigat, hindi po sila freeloader.

**[00:49:38.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000726)  
At yan po yung essential ng universal healthcare.

**[00:49:40.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000727)  
To the Department of Health, last year po, nung ating budget hearing, nabanggit po ninyo, nabanggit po ng Department of Health,

**[00:49:48.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000728)  
na mayroon pang 1,823 health centers dahil hindi pa natatapos.

**[00:49:56.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000729)  
At ang pag-kompleto sa kanila would require 32.4 billion.

**[00:50:00.600] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000730)  
Ang tanong po natin sa PhilHealth, ilang health centers na nakompleto po this year, Mr. Secretary?

**[00:50:05.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000731)  
Super Health Centers.

**[00:50:11.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000732)  
Sa datos po namin, ito po yung Super Health Centers.

**[00:50:17.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000733)  
Sa datos po namin, ito po yung Super Health Centers.

**[00:50:18.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000734)  
Sa dados po namin, ito po yung Super Health Centers.

**[00:50:19.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000735)  
muna po, habang kinukuha po natin yung mga regular Rhu.

**[00:50:24.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000736)  
Ang total po na HCF funded na super health center ay 806 as of September 2025.

**[00:50:34.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000737)  
Ang completed na po ay 484.

**[00:50:37.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000738)  
Ang ready for occupancy po ay 241 out of which 222 na po ang operational at 19 ang non operational.

**[00:50:46.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000739)  
For various reasons.

**[00:50:47.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000740)  
For various reasons po, kaya nga po kinakailangan din pong mag-ugnay doon sa mga local government para po ano po ang problema at matulungan sila para maging operational.

**[00:50:59.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000741)  
Yung pong partially completed ay 224, of which 14 po yung operational na kahit po partially completed at 210 po ang non-operational.

**[00:51:11.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000742)  
So kung tutuusin po, pagsamahin po natin yung ready for occupancy at saka yung partially completed.

**[00:51:16.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000743)  
229 po ang non-operational. At ako nga po ay humihingi ng mga tala o tali po nito kasi napag-usapan din po namin na papano po ituloy at para po mabigyan po nga ng tulong ito pong local government para po ma-operationalize po ito.

**[00:51:38.140] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000744)  
Ano po ang ibig sabihin itong 229 na non-operational?

**[00:51:42.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000745)  
So yun pong 229.

**[00:51:46.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000746)  
229 pong non-operational ay hindi pa po nabibigyan ng license to operate for various reasons po kasi hindi pa po compliant doon sa standards na nire-require po ng ating licensing bureau.

**[00:52:00.620] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000747)  
Maraming salamat Mr. Secretary. Ilang health centers po ang target nating makumpleto by next year at magkano po ang proposed 2027 budget para po makumpleto ang mga ito?

**[00:52:10.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000748)  
So sa kabuan po.

**[00:52:25.050] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000749)  
Ah, Cong, minority speaker... minority leader. Sorry po.

**[00:52:35.270] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000750)  
Wag nyo po akong promote Mr. Secretary.

**[00:52:37.610] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000751)  
Ang ating pong tina-target ay 176 primary care facilities for 2027 na may kabuong pong 506 million na budget.

**[00:52:49.930] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000752)  
Salamat po. Kaya ako po ito natanong sapagkat medyo malaking halaga po.

**[00:52:55.050] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000753)  
Ito at meron po tayong COA findings.

**[00:52:57.770] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000754)  
They identified 14.52 billion in delayed or unimplemented projects with 9 contracts at the central office amounting to 8.61 billion pesos.

**[00:53:08.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000755)  
Ano po ang ginagawa ng Departamento ng Reforma in response to the COA findings po Mr. Chair?

**[00:53:15.310] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000756)  
So nagkaroon po tayo ng omnibus guidelines po para sa HFEP.

**[00:53:19.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000757)  
At kung gusto po ng mga miyembro natin kumuha po ng kopya ay nandi dito po.

**[00:53:25.050] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000758)  
Ito po ay sineset na po yung mga preconditions bago po natin maisama sa ating budget.

**[00:53:31.170] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000759)  
So dapat po dyan yung site conditions ay maayos.

**[00:53:34.150] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000760)  
Ang titulo ng mga lupa ay nai-transfer na doon sa proponent.

**[00:53:38.470] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000761)  
Meron pong commitment doon sa health resource na health manpower resource ang ating pong local government.

**[00:53:45.610] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000762)  
Yung pong design ng kanila pong mga local health centers ay maayos.

**[00:53:50.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000763)  
At pati po yung kanilang parang matching grant o commitment.

**[00:53:54.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000764)  
Para po i-power up yung ating mga RHUs po ay nando doon po.

**[00:54:00.110] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000765)  
So yun po ay hinihingi namin at isa nga pong reason kaya marami pong hindi rin namin nadadownloadan kahit po ito ay nasa allocation.

**[00:54:08.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000766)  
Ay hindi po handa yung ating mga local government units na magbigay po nitong mga site conditions.

**[00:54:14.650] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000767)  
Maraming salamat at isa po ito doon sa mga areas in the implementation of the GAA for next year na talaga pong babatayan natin.

**[00:54:21.370] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000768)  
Tumawid naman po tayo sa topic ng HIV.

**[00:54:24.730] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000769)  
Sana po itong itatanong ko ng mga taga-PINAC.

**[00:54:27.910] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000770)  
Sinisilip ko po yung ating mga targets ng NHSP ng 2027.

**[00:54:34.750] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000771)  
At tinitinan ko rin po yung gaps.

**[00:54:37.570] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000772)  
Particularly po halimbawa as basic as condoms.

**[00:54:41.890] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000773)  
Kailangan po natin 1.2 million condom beneficiaries would require up to 302.4 million annually.

**[00:54:48.510] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000774)  
Lubrikans po.

**[00:54:49.810] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000775)  
Kailangan natin ang shortfall po ay up to 98.7 million.

**[00:54:54.730] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000776)  
So medyo glaring po yung gaps.

**[00:54:57.010] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000777)  
So even HIV self-testing, ang shortfall po ay nasa 26 to 63.9 million na shortfall.

**[00:55:04.390] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000778)  
So ano pong ginagawa natin given na domestic funding na po karamihan na majority po natin sa HIV procurement?

**[00:55:11.850] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000779)  
How will the Department of Health ensure that the 2027 budget is actually sufficient to meet the NHSP targets?

**[00:55:19.570] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000780)  
Mr. Chair.

**[00:55:20.150] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000781)  
Please wind up.

**[00:55:22.450] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000782)  
The Deputy Minority Leaders.

**[00:55:24.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000783)  
So una po talaga medyo po yung aming procurement kailangan po talaga mag-improve.

**[00:55:32.110] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000784)  
Kasi yung pong commodities ang primary ano po dyan eh para ma-reach natin itong ating mga targets.

**[00:55:38.430] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000785)  
Yung isa po rin po ay papalawakin na po natin sa PhilHealth yung ating coverage po ng OHAT kaya na nasabi ko kanina.

**[00:55:46.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000786)  
To include PrEP.

**[00:55:47.630] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000787)  
To include even the testing.

**[00:55:50.110] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000788)  
So 2 prong po through YACAP.

**[00:55:51.990] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000789)  
Through the OHAT.

**[00:55:53.410] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000790)  
Through the OHAT.

**[00:55:54.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000791)  
Program.

**[00:55:55.930] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000792)  
And then DOH po talagang dito sa commodities.

**[00:55:59.790] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000793)  
Mabilis lamang po for the last question.

**[00:56:01.790] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000794)  
Salamat po at tanagaabang ating mga key population.

**[00:56:04.830] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000795)  
Pag-usapan po natin yung supply and chain management kasi nababalitaan po namin ang daming failure sa ating logistics.

**[00:56:10.910] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000796)  
Last question Mr. Chair.

**[00:56:12.530] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000797)  
Bakit po sa ating NEP pare-parehong 10 million na allocation para sa lahat ng regions para ting sa supply and chain management?

**[00:56:19.090] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000798)  
10 million sa NCR, 10 million sa Central Visayas, 10 million sa Caraga.

**[00:56:22.850] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000799)  
E given po yung geographic.

**[00:56:24.730] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000800)  
Character of these regions, sigurado po tayo hindi pare-pareho yung requirements when it comes to logistics.

**[00:56:30.570] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000801)  
Ano pong science behind this 10 million allocation across all regions?

**[00:56:34.790] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000802)  
Okay, that will be the last question.

**[00:56:37.290] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000803)  
DML, Secretary, please respond.

**[00:56:40.590] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000804)  
Nakita ko rin po yun bakit pare-pareho.

**[00:56:42.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000805)  
Iba-iba naman yung site, yung geographic layout nung kada region.

**[00:56:47.890] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000806)  
Pero ang sabi po ay dahil sa warehousing which is common daw for all the regions.

**[00:56:52.450] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000807)  
Pero gaya po nang nasabi ko.

**[00:56:54.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000808)  
Nag-aaralan din namin po paano nga po ito maging mas efficient.

**[00:56:58.950] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000809)  
At ano po yung variability factors na kailangan po nating i-consider.

**[00:57:03.430] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000810)  
E nababother po kasi tayo sa mga ganyang klase na allocation kasi sigurado po tayo hindi yan batay doon sa evidence at pangangailangan ng region.

**[00:57:10.210] Rep. Percival "Perci" Cendaña** (asr-000811)  
Salamat Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:11.570] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000812)  
Maraming salamat DML Sendanya for your very valuable and significant questions.

**[00:57:17.670] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000813)  
We now move to the majority. May I recognize Representative Adrian.

**[00:57:24.730] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000814)  
Amatong.

**[00:57:25.230] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000815)  
Thank you very much Mr. Chair.

**[00:57:27.230] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000816)  
Please proceed.

**[00:57:27.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000817)  
Good morning po sa buong DOH family.

**[00:57:29.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000818)  
Gusto ko lang pong itanong kasi pag may nakakausap akong mga legislators sa ibang bansa lagi sila nagugulat sa healthcare system natin.

**[00:57:39.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000819)  
Kasi meron tayong sa PhilHealth, meron tayong may yakap at saka ano-ano pang mga programa.

**[00:57:45.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000820)  
Bakit po hindi natin mapag-isa so that kung mga kasi minsan may mga programs tayo na hindi na nga maintindihan ng mga constituents natin eh.

**[00:57:53.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000821)  
Ako nga minsan may ibang hindi ko maintindihan eh.

**[00:57:55.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000822)  
So bakit po hindi nalang isang healthcare system, isang programa na kung saan malaki yung nakukuha ng mga constituents natin?

**[00:58:03.730] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000823)  
Ba't ang gulo-gulo pa po?

**[00:58:05.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000824)  
Tama po yan dahil sa pag-aaral nga po ng Philippine Institute of Developmental Studies.

**[00:58:12.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000825)  
Kapag ang payment po ay siloed, may iba-ibang pinagkukunan. May PhilHealth, may galing po sa DOH, may galing po sa DSWD-IX.

**[00:58:22.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000826)  
Humihina po yung leverage ng atin.

**[00:58:25.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000827)  
Kung ito po ay ang mandato ng mga laman po mag-cover at magbayad ay PhilHealth.

**[00:58:33.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000828)  
Kung ito po ay unti-unti nating ma-ecentralize, yung leverage po natin, halimbawa na magbago po yung payment natin to what you call prospective, parang pakyawan.

**[00:58:44.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000829)  
Inumpisahan na po namin, kaya po maaaring nabalitaan nyo may mga private hospital na po na naglalaanong kama nila sa NBB.

**[00:58:52.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000830)  
Yun po kasi nagagamit na po namin.

**[00:58:54.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000831)  
Nagagamit na po namin yung ating purchasing leverage and purchasing power.

**[00:58:58.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000832)  
Pero ngayon po, pag siloed po ang payment, medyo mahina po yun.

**[00:59:02.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000833)  
Kaya nga po, unti-unti na namin pinag-aaralan paano po i-consolidate under PhilHealth.

**[00:59:07.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000834)  
Obviously, nabanggit nyo pa, may mga studies.

**[00:59:11.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000835)  
Can we work together so that we can come up with a solution so that we can consolidate everything and make it easy for our constituents?

**[00:59:18.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000836)  
Kasi kawawa, naguguluhan sila minsan eh. Tapos minsan ang tagal pang maningil.

**[00:59:22.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000837)  
Ang second question ko po.

**[00:59:23.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000838)  
Sa PhilHealth.

**[00:59:24.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000839)  
Kasi yung mga constituents ko, lagi silang nagtatanong, bayad kami ng bayad sa PhilHealth.

**[00:59:29.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000840)  
Pero pag na-hospital kami, konting-konti naman yung nakukuha namin sa PhilHealth.

**[00:59:33.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000841)  
In fact, napag-usapan before eh.

**[00:59:35.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000842)  
And I also agree na dapat hindi nakalodge ang mga politiko ang medical assistance kasi minsan napo-politika.

**[00:59:42.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000843)  
As much as we want that to happen, ang problema kasi, pag lumalapit sa amin yung constituents namin,

**[00:59:48.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000844)  
kapiranggot lang yung sinasagot ng PhilHealth, eh syempre ipaglalaban namin na pwede rin naming matulungan.

**[00:59:52.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000845)  
Di ba po?

**[00:59:53.240] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000846)  
Sir, in the last two years po, halos doble na po yung...

**[01:00:00.000] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000847)  
naging coverage ng PhilHealth.

**[01:00:01.520] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000848)  
But we recognize, for certain cases,

**[01:00:04.260] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000849)  
lalo na yung mga pasyente na mas malubha yung kondisyon,

**[01:00:08.180] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000850)  
kulang pa rin yung bayad doon.

**[01:00:10.160] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000851)  
So meron pong parte ng pinag-aaralan namin sa benepisyo namin,

**[01:00:14.180] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000852)  
yung tinatawag na DRG or may modifier po.

**[01:00:17.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000853)  
Ngayon po, kahit ikaw ay may high blood,

**[01:00:20.620] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000854)  
60 years old na kunwari, na mas maraming sakit,

**[01:00:24.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000855)  
kung nagka-say dengue ka, same lang po yung bayad ng PhilHealth sa inyo

**[01:00:28.320] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000856)  
versus someone na very young and healthy.

**[01:00:31.660] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000857)  
Parang yun yung klarong-klaro na dapat medyo magkaiba po

**[01:00:35.240] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000858)  
or may adjustments po yung bayad.

**[01:00:36.920] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000859)  
So meron na pong pag-aaral na nangyayari doon.

**[01:00:39.940] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000860)  
And that is supposed to inform how we adjust the payments further.

**[01:00:44.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000861)  
Second, sir, is clearly we have to work with the public hospitals

**[01:00:49.720] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000862)  
whose 90% of bids should really be no balance billing.

**[01:00:54.000] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000863)  
And merong mechanism po yung mga public hospitals

**[01:00:57.080] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000864)  
to get additional budget from LGU as well as additional MAIFIP, etc.,

**[01:01:01.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000865)  
for example, na dapat at the end of the day,

**[01:01:04.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000866)  
nakaka-uwi yung pasyente na wala po siyang extra na binayad.

**[01:01:08.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000867)  
So I think those are clearly implementation challenges

**[01:01:12.480] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000868)  
that we have to work with on top of rates adjustment

**[01:01:16.520] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000869)  
that we'll have to work on.

**[01:01:17.740] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000870)  
But we definitely agree with you, sir, na lalo na now that also the premium is higher,

**[01:01:24.920] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000871)  
our members expect na mas malakas,

**[01:01:27.080] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000872)  
kaya din naman talaga yung coverage.

**[01:01:29.400] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000873)  
Na-appreciate ko po yung sagot nyo.

**[01:01:31.560] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000874)  
Ang problema ko lang po kasi,

**[01:01:33.740] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000875)  
pag nakikita ng mga constituents natin,

**[01:01:36.260] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000876)  
na example yung sa PhilHealth Board,

**[01:01:38.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000877)  
pag nakita nila yung benefits na nakakuha ng mga members ng board,

**[01:01:41.700] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000878)  
sabi, ang lalaki.

**[01:01:42.960] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000879)  
Tapos pag narinig nila na may surplus na, may savings na ang PhilHealth,

**[01:01:47.400] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000880)  
kaya kinuha daw ng national government,

**[01:01:49.460] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000881)  
hindi na naman itin yan.

**[01:01:50.320] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000882)  
Paano nagkakaroon ng surplus?

**[01:01:51.900] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000883)  
Eh, hindi naman kami natutulungan ng maayos.

**[01:01:54.160] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000884)  
That is something that is very, very hard to explain,

**[01:01:57.080] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000885)  
to those na nangangailangan.

**[01:01:58.820] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000886)  
Lalo na pag pag-need na nila, pag na-hospital siya,

**[01:02:00.980] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000887)  
tapos kapiranggot lang.

**[01:02:02.520] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000888)  
Tapos they end up begging from politicians for help.

**[01:02:05.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000889)  
Kaya nga sa office namin, tinanggal namin yung,

**[01:02:08.100] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000890)  
don't talk to me.

**[01:02:09.420] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000891)  
Ang staff natin, pag nakita nangangailangan kayo,

**[01:02:11.380] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000892)  
tutulungan kayo agad.

**[01:02:12.700] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000893)  
I hate it when constituents go to politicians and need to beg.

**[01:02:17.580] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000894)  
Parang nakakawalan ng dignidad.

**[01:02:21.180] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000895)  
Unfortunately, hindi lang natin kaya pondohan lahat.

**[01:02:23.500] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000896)  
Kaya nga po sana may mas maayos na sistema so that PhilHealth,

**[01:02:26.920] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000897)  
they can fund as much as they can.

**[01:02:30.240] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000898)  
And then, definitely sir, we know that the hospital bill is partly made up of

**[01:02:34.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000899)  
hospital fees and professional fees.

**[01:02:37.440] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000900)  
So, alam din po namin sa lahat ng discussions with our legislators na

**[01:02:42.240] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000901)  
the PF is also a big challenge.

**[01:02:44.960] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000902)  
And so, Sec. Edwin has already started and we will continue

**[01:02:50.040] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000903)  
our discussions po with the professional societies.

**[01:02:54.060] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000904)  
Lalo na kung magbigyan natin ng PhilHealth,

**[01:02:56.920] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000905)  
plus more predictable, ma-EFIP,

**[01:02:59.880] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000906)  
ano yung ways for the healthcare professionals to make their PFs din

**[01:03:05.160] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000907)  
a little bit more predictable, diba?

**[01:03:07.280] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000908)  
Para hindi tayo lahat magkagulatan kung magkano yun.

**[01:03:11.440] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000909)  
And siguro lastly, sir, we also recognize na

**[01:03:15.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000910)  
while a lot of improvements have been done the last 18 months

**[01:03:19.520] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000911)  
under Sec. Edwin and PhilHealth,

**[01:03:21.620] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000912)  
we also know that many of the providers sometimes,

**[01:03:25.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000913)  
because mahirap yung process,

**[01:03:26.920] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000914)  
hindi na nila ginagamit yung PhilHealth to enroll kung ano yung condition ng patient.

**[01:03:34.620] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000915)  
So, ma-EFIP na lang gagamitin nila kasi mas mahirap yung proseso.

**[01:03:38.440] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000916)  
So, yun din naman po yung commitment namin to make it easier for healthcare providers

**[01:03:43.400] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000917)  
to be part of the benefit packages that we have

**[01:03:47.100] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000918)  
and also to make our frontline services for them much easier.

**[01:03:52.080] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000919)  
Thank you po.

**[01:03:53.080] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000920)  
Nabanggit po kanina yung sayakap.

**[01:03:55.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000921)  
Tapos, may mga kalukuhan na naman sa YACAP eh.

**[01:03:58.140] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000922)  
Yung mga LGUs, kasi sabi niyo nga minsan na-e-enroll yung mga tao na hindi nga nila alam eh.

**[01:04:03.260] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000923)  
Tapos, nagagamit yung pondo na intended for them na hindi nila alam eh.

**[01:04:06.680] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000924)  
So, ano po yung safeguards natin dito?

**[01:04:11.880] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000925)  
So, definitely, sir, we will be coming up with our parang YACAP 2.0 enhanced policies

**[01:04:19.540] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000926)  
and that's a result of all the consultations and all the feedback that's coming from the ground.

**[01:04:25.140] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000927)  
So, for example,

**[01:04:26.540] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000928)  
part of this would include really institutionalizing yung liveness check,

**[01:04:32.720] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000929)  
meaning may verification na yung patient, biometrics,

**[01:04:37.920] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000930)  
para po malaman na yun yung actual patient.

**[01:04:40.280] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000931)  
Second is, mas malalaman nung member kung ginamit ba siya.

**[01:04:45.300] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000932)  
So, ngayon po, makikita nyo sa EGOV app na may YACAP provider na kayo, etc.

**[01:04:51.140] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000933)  
Nalalagay po dyan kung may claim kayo sa inpatient.

**[01:04:54.660] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000934)  
So, soon enough, ilalagay natin.

**[01:04:56.580] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000935)  
So, ito nga din po namin kung yung provider ay nag-claim on your behalf dahil YACAP mo sila.

**[01:05:03.900] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000936)  
So, kung hindi mo pala alam, then actually malalaman din ng pasyente, no?

**[01:05:08.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000937)  
So, many other similar mechanisms will be put in place as well as CAP

**[01:05:14.760] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000938)  
in terms of ano lang yung kaya ng isang provider na alagaan.

**[01:05:20.020] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000939)  
So, kunwari po ngayon, medyo may default tayo, everything can go through the LGU.

**[01:05:26.420] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000940)  
But,

**[01:05:26.540] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000941)  
But as we move forward, we want to be able to discuss with each province, citywide health system

**[01:05:33.460] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000942)  
and say, ngayon po ang kapasidad po ng probinsya ninyo ay alagaan sa 300,000 of the 500,000 constituents.

**[01:05:42.900] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000943)  
Ano pong gagawin natin dun sa 200,000 remaining?

**[01:05:46.640] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000944)  
Tate po, pilitin natin sa lahat ng health center nyo pa rin sila ma-assign.

**[01:05:51.960] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000945)  
Pero kung hindi po sila ma-accommodate, unfair naman yun sa pasyente.

**[01:05:56.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000946)  
So we want to work with the LGUs, not to force them to become healthcare providers

**[01:06:05.580] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000947)  
but to get them to be the conveners of the providers in their locality.

**[01:06:11.060] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000948)  
And say, itong 200,000 na ito, meron bang private non-for-profit na pwedeng tumulong sa kanila

**[01:06:17.960] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000949)  
to operate certain yakap centers in the interim and maybe in two years time

**[01:06:23.840] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000950)  
with HFEP, with HealthU,

**[01:06:26.540] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000951)  
with Human Resource Support, kaya na ni LGU yun, babalik na po yung mga pasyente nun kay LGU.

**[01:06:32.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000952)  
So para lang din po fair sa pasyente na where PhilHealth is paying for their services,

**[01:06:38.900] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000953)  
they should get the right services po.

**[01:06:42.160] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000954)  
Meron po kami na reklamo dati na mayor sa amin kasi dun sa PhilHealth program,

**[01:06:47.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000955)  
ang ginawa niya, he gathered a lot of people dun sa hospital niya,

**[01:06:51.260] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000956)  
tapos pinakain, pinabaunan, pinapirma, tuwan-tuwan yung mga tao,

**[01:06:56.540] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000957)  
pala nila, natulungan sila.

**[01:06:58.420] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000958)  
Pag uwi nila, yung PhilHealth pala nila, ano na, sagad na.

**[01:07:02.840] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000959)  
Tapos, ang problema ko lang po sa PhilHealth, hindi naman nila hinabol yung mayor ngayon.

**[01:07:08.120] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000960)  
So ngayon, mukhang sa AKAP, mukhang yun din ang mangyayari eh.

**[01:07:12.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000961)  
So I hope yung grievance mechanism natin is designed in such a way na

**[01:07:16.020] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000962)  
hahabulin talaga natin yung mga taong umaabuso.

**[01:07:20.620] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000963)  
Kasi ang sama ng logo sa PhilHealth eh, kasi kaya nga nakabalik na mayor yun ngayon

**[01:07:24.340] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000964)  
kasi hindi naman nila hinabol.

**[01:07:25.780] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000965)  
Sinara yung hospital.

**[01:07:26.540] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000966)  
Yung hospital niya, head ng hospital niya, pinanagot.

**[01:07:29.520] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000967)  
Pero yung pinaka-utak nung ginawa, eh hindi naman pinanagot.

**[01:07:58.780] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000968)  
Spirit and individual, dun po sila lahat.

**[01:08:01.580] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000969)  
Yun yung parang entry point.

**[01:08:02.760] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000970)  
So we have to do it well because that will be the entry point for Filipinos and to the health system po.

**[01:08:08.800] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-000971)  
Thank you.

**[01:08:10.260] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000972)  
I'd like to understand more yung programa ng AKAP po

**[01:08:13.680] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000973)  
and why ito yung parang, kinasabi niyong base program natin para ma-integrate lahat.

**[01:08:19.340] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000974)  
Because if there is anything we can do to legislate for that, please, please tell us.

**[01:08:25.720] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000975)  
Kasi we are desperate to find a way na,

**[01:08:28.780] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000976)  
para makaawa yung mga constituents natin for medical assistance.

**[01:08:32.500] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000977)  
Kasi kawawang-kawawa na nga sila.

**[01:08:34.760] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000978)  
Tapos parang nakakawala ng dignidad kasi yung kailangan pang magmakaawa para matulungan.

**[01:08:39.740] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000979)  
Sana po magtulungan tayo para at par naman tayo sa ibang mga bansa.

**[01:08:43.720] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000980)  
Kasi pinagtatawa naman tayo minsan eh.

**[01:08:45.240] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000981)  
May nakausap po two weeks ago sa lihan kong conference.

**[01:08:49.200] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000982)  
Sabi nila, bakit ka pa may medical assistance?

**[01:08:52.280] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000983)  
Eh yung healthcare system yun.

**[01:08:53.860] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000984)  
Wala akong masagot eh.

**[01:08:55.200] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000985)  
Kasi ako mismo parang hindi ko maintindihan yung healthcare system natin.

**[01:08:58.780] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000986)  
Magtulungan tayo.

**[01:08:59.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000987)  
Sana po ma-solve natin yun.

**[01:09:01.500] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000988)  
Ang last ko po is, sana i-remind natin yung mga hospitals na,

**[01:09:06.120] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000989)  
di ba dapat ang first contact ng mga pasyente is yung medical social worker.

**[01:09:10.680] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000990)  
May mga hospitals na hindi yung medical social worker yung unang nakakausap nila eh.

**[01:09:14.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000991)  
Kaya prone tuloy sa mga abuses ng mga kalukohan minsan ng doctors or ng mga hospitals.

**[01:09:21.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000992)  
Okay, please wind up, Congressman Amatong.

**[01:09:25.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000993)  
Phil Health, DOH, please reply.

**[01:09:28.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000994)  
Thank you.

**[01:09:28.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000995)  
Yes po eh, kasi kailangan nga merong skills to assess yung capability or yung social status ng ating pasyente.

**[01:09:38.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000996)  
Kasi nga po doon sa pag-seset nga po ng rules ng ano naman po, MIFIP, pinag-usapan na po namin, may iba pong hospital na may classification ABCDE, depende po doon sa social class, yung dami po nung pwedeng ibigay ng MIFIP.

**[01:09:53.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000997)  
So ano po namin yan, i-institutionalize po namin.

**[01:09:57.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-000998)  
At dapat po yung talagang trained na mga social worker or at least po merong certification ang pwedeng magbigay ng assessment.

**[01:10:05.460] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-000999)  
Since may time ba po, ang last issue ko po, ang DOH minsan, mga regional offices nyo, pag nagre-reklamo kami tungkol sa bad practices ng mga hospitals,

**[01:10:13.400] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001000)  
lagi nilang sinasabing na wala silang position na sawayin yung mga hospitals.

**[01:10:17.600] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001001)  
Sana naman po, meron din kayong ano na pwedeng yung sitahin yung mga hospitals who are not doing well.

**[01:10:22.180] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001002)  
Because, so umpisa pa lang, if they're not doing well, mas mabuti nang masita natin para masolve natin kaagad.

**[01:10:27.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001003)  
Sige po, actually po, meron kaming corporate action center sa PhilHealth na yun po yung pinagdudulugan ng mga reklamo at meron na po po kaming sistema ngayon pa paano po i-triage, kumbaga.

**[01:10:42.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001004)  
Mag-uusap po kami na gamitin din po yung platform na yun for hospitals.

**[01:10:46.660] Rep. Adrian Michael A. Amatong** (asr-001005)  
Maraming salamat po. Maraming salamat din po sa tulong nyo sa Liloid General Hospital. Thank you.

**[01:10:50.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001006)  
Salamat din po.

**[01:10:51.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001007)  
Maraming salamat, Congressman Amatong.

**[01:10:55.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001008)  
And, Secretary, may hotline po ba?

**[01:10:58.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001009)  
May hotline po yung inyong central response center para mayaman po ng lahat kung may mga complaint?

**[01:11:11.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001010)  
Alam ko po yung 1555. 1555 po.

**[01:11:15.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001011)  
So, any complaints sa hospitals, public and private, pwede tumawag sa 1555.

**[01:11:25.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001012)  
Pero yun nga po, parang meron lang po kaming ilang mga seats.

**[01:11:31.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001013)  
Kaya gusto namin i-professionalize.

**[01:11:33.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001014)  
I-professionalize na isa talagang call center, kahit third party yung mag-engage, na nabibigyan kami ng datos po.

**[01:11:40.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001015)  
Kaya nag-uusap na po kami pa paano pag-isahin.

**[01:11:43.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001016)  
So, I think everyone is looking forward to the efficient operation of that response center, yung call center ng 1555.

**[01:11:54.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001017)  
So, hopefully, dumating tayo doon para immediate yung maging response ng DOH at ng PhilHealth.

**[01:12:02.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001018)  
Thank you.

**[01:12:03.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001019)  
By the way, for the information of everyone, nilagay po natin sa special provision ng 2026 budget na pwede pong 100% sagutin ng professional fee, aside from the hospital bill.

**[01:12:17.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001020)  
Nasa special provision po yan ng 2026 GAA para po alam ng lahat.

**[01:12:24.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001021)  
So, kailangan lang po natin siguro magkaroon ng number one, patanggap sa mga doctors, especially in the private sector.

**[01:12:33.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001022)  
Minsan hindi nila tinatanggap, or most of the time.

**[01:12:36.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001023)  
And yung CAP, kasi minsan nagiging runaway hospital bill and professional fee.

**[01:12:42.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001024)  
So, I think we should work on that.

**[01:12:45.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001025)  
Ika may po, may nilabas po si former Secretary Ted niya po, Herbosa, na bagamat nandun po, nakapaloob doon sa 2026 yung 100% PF, ay maglalabas po kami nung mga patakaran.

**[01:13:00.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001026)  
Kung ito po ay tinitingnan namin, ipapattern ba namin?

**[01:13:03.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001027)  
HMO, na meron kang RVU times multiple, or ito ba ay percentage of what PhilHealth is covering, X numbers of times.

**[01:13:11.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001028)  
So, yun po yung aming pinag-aaral.

**[01:13:14.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001029)  
Otherwise po, inflationary, nagdoble, nagtitriple po yung PF.

**[01:13:17.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001030)  
Yes, understood, Secretary. Maraming salamat. We will wait for the guidelines.

**[01:13:22.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001031)  
Thank you.

**[01:13:22.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001032)  
So, we move back to the minority. May we recognize Assistant Minority Leader, Rep. Iris Montes.

**[01:13:34.450] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001033)  
Ma'am, please proceed.

**[01:13:35.370] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001034)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[01:13:36.230] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001035)  
You have five minutes.

**[01:13:37.630] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001036)  
Magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[01:13:40.130] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001037)  
On November 7 last year, this representation took the floor to deliver a privileged speech on the WHO's Global Tuberculosis Report for 2025.

**[01:13:52.530] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001038)  
The report confronted us with the undeniable truth that we have consistently ranked fifth in the world in terms of TB ranking, third in 2025, third.

**[01:14:03.690] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001039)  
And 100 Filipinos dying every single day because of TB.

**[01:14:09.950] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001040)  
I know, Mr. Secretary, that you have not warmed your seat yet.

**[01:14:13.390] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001041)  
But can you please inform us, what exactly did you inherit regarding the procurement of TB drugs?

**[01:14:27.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001042)  
Yung anti-TB drugs nga po, at saka yung cartridges para dun sa gene experts.

**[01:14:35.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001043)  
Ay talaga pong medyo na-delay yung procurement.

**[01:14:39.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001044)  
Kaya nung nire-constitute nga namin yung back, medyo binigyan ko ng directive to really push.

**[01:14:45.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001045)  
Simultaneously po, nag-reach out kami dun sa mga ating mga foreign grant na pwedeng magbigay muna habang inaayos namin itong procurement.

**[01:14:58.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001046)  
At may nagre-respond naman po.

**[01:15:00.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001047)  
In fact, the Global Fund already committed to give us substantially, almost $159 million worth of medicines for TB, HIV, and malaria.

**[01:15:12.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001048)  
Bulk of it will be in TB.

**[01:15:14.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001049)  
Ang issue lang po namin ay paano po ayusin na for the first time po, binigyan po ang DOH as procuring agent.

**[01:15:22.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001050)  
Hindi na po dadaan ng private third party.

**[01:15:25.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001051)  
So kami po ay nag-ano kung paano po ayusin yung taxes.

**[01:15:30.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001052)  
As I said, since we're already agency to agency, meron naman po kami pag-uusap initially ng DOF na i-relax po yung requirement na magbayad pa ng taxes kung agency to agency.

**[01:15:41.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001053)  
So yun po yung inaayos.

**[01:15:44.770] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001054)  
Yung current procurement timelines po, ang bid evaluation ay September 3 to 10, post-qual po ng September 11 to 22.

**[01:15:51.870] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001055)  
At sana po ay ma-recommend na po for awarding by end September po.

**[01:15:59.570] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001056)  
Mr. Secretary, have you already...

**[01:16:01.950] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001057)  
Have you already conducted some kind of a review kung ano po ba ang nangyari ng nakaraan, nagkaroon ng failure of bidding, ano po ba ang naging problema that we were not able to procure the much needed TB drugs?

**[01:16:18.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001058)  
So ang inaayos ko lang po muna yung prospective kasi yung kailangan po nating bigyan po ng solusyon.

**[01:16:25.330] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001059)  
Pero yun po ay binigay ko na po dun sa ating procurement service under USEC sa Kilabon na magbigay po ng recommendation.

**[01:16:31.950] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001060)  
Kung ano nga po yung nangyari.

**[01:16:34.290] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001061)  
When can we expect po kaya na ma-deliver yung mga TB drugs and all the needed equipment sa mga gene experts natin?

**[01:16:44.830] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001062)  
So kung ma-award po by end of September, another one to two months po. So by end of October or by November.

**[01:16:51.710] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001063)  
Sa 2027 budget po, did we include again budget for the procurement of TB drugs?

**[01:17:02.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001064)  
Yes po. This is on top of the global...

**[01:17:05.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001065)  
Global fund, ano po. Meron po tayo na 3.02 billion allocated for TB.

**[01:17:14.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001066)  
At nga ang isa rin po namin pinofocus are the for community-based setting, yung pinaka-congested na pinagmumula na maraming drug resistance at TB, which are people with PDLs.

**[01:17:25.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001067)  
Mga people with...

**[01:17:28.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001068)  
Ano nga yung liberty with...

**[01:17:33.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001069)  
Tsaka po yung mga congested...

**[01:17:35.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001070)  
Composted areas like workplace na tabi-tabi. So yun po yung ating pinofocus na magawan po kaagad ng screening.

**[01:17:51.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001071)  
Isa pa rin po palang quick solution somehow, yung pong TB na pwede na pong i-avail sa gamot.

**[01:18:00.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001072)  
Kaya meron po tayong gamot. For first-liner lang po yun, yung mga usual na triple, quadruple anti-cox or anti-TB meds.

**[01:18:09.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001073)  
Pero yun pong for...

**[01:18:12.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001074)  
MDR, multi-drug resistance, tsaka XDR, DOH po yun. Kaya kasama po yun sa nakabudget.

**[01:18:18.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001075)  
So sa DOH, yung procurement mo, meron for MDR and XDR.

**[01:18:22.800] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001076)  
Yes.

**[01:18:23.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001077)  
Nabanggit niyo po, Secretary, yung tungkol sa Global Fund, no? So kayo na po ang directly magpo-procure, hindi na dadaan sa private sector?

**[01:18:34.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001078)  
O this time lang po? Ngayon lang kayo pinagbigyan?

**[01:18:38.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001079)  
First time po na ginawa po yan.

**[01:18:41.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001080)  
Kaya we're working on the...

**[01:18:42.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001081)  
Yung framework. Kahapon nga lang po, pinirmahan ko na yung how to move forward on discussion of the framework agreement.

**[01:18:48.600] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001082)  
But is it true that yung Global Fund po natin is tapering off consistently through the years? Tama po, di ba?

**[01:18:57.540] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001083)  
So ibig sabihin po, if it's tapering off, kailangan makompensate natin from our domestic financing to be able to compensate for whatever is lost. Tama po, Mr. Secretary?

**[01:19:09.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001084)  
Yes po. Kaya nga while we're transitioning.

**[01:19:12.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001085)  
Palakas na ng PhilHealth yung coverage niya ng TB. Kasi doon po magta-transition.

**[01:19:16.900] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001086)  
So maraming salamat po. So in two months, we will be able to procure the TB drugs.

**[01:19:22.300] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001087)  
Moving on po, on vaccines procurement. For 2026 po, ba did we procure vaccines? For instance, for influenza, pneumococcal vaccines, meron po ba tayong procurement?

**[01:19:41.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001088)  
Yung pong mga vaccines natin, actually carry over pa ng 2025. Kaya po ngayon lang na move.

**[01:19:49.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001089)  
So delayed nga po, pero pinahabol din po rin po yung 2026. Doon po sa mga flu vaccine, kasi depende nga po yung doon sa kung ano po yung prevailing strain during the year, pwede pa naman po daw yung 2025 gamitin nga yung 2026.

**[01:20:10.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001090)  
So meron na po tayong na-award na pentavalent para doon sa mga bata at para doon sa immunization po ng pentavalent na mga bata.

**[01:20:19.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001091)  
At saka ang flu vaccine pa ang pending, pero nabigyan na natin yung pneumococcal. Pneumococcal po ang na-award na rin po namin.

**[01:20:34.020] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001092)  
Ang concern ko lang po kasi Mr. Secretary, moving forward, sana timely yung ating procurement katulad po ngayon, flu season na naman.

**[01:20:42.040] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001093)  
Dapat by this time, we should have rolled out our influenza vaccines because if not, parang sayang din po.

**[01:20:49.620] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001094)  
So moving on.

**[01:20:55.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-001095)

**[01:21:07.140] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001096)

**[02:00:17.300] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-002114)

**[02:00:47.360] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-002117)

**[02:01:08.940] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-002119)

**[02:01:56.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002127)

**[02:02:09.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002129)

**[02:03:18.110] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002138)

**[02:03:21.470] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002139)

**[02:03:25.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002140)

**[02:03:28.130] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002141)

**[02:03:30.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002142)  
Rizal Medical Center.

**[02:03:32.590] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002143)

**[02:03:37.370] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002144)

**[02:03:44.170] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002145)  
112 million pesos ang binawas.

**[02:03:54.550] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002146)

**[02:03:57.030] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002147)

**[02:04:06.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002148)

**[02:04:08.310] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002149)

**[02:04:20.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002151)

**[02:04:32.350] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002155)

**[02:04:32.890] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002156)

**[02:04:38.870] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002157)

**[02:04:44.190] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002158)

**[02:04:45.570] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002159)  
Yung hindi pa...

**[02:04:46.650] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002160)

**[02:04:49.610] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002161)

**[02:04:52.190] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002162)  
So, yung mga unfilled nyo, tinanggal na.

**[02:04:55.250] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002163)  
So, nabawasan kayo ng plantilla positions.

**[02:04:58.150] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002164)

**[02:05:05.750] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002165)

**[02:05:13.770] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002166)

**[02:05:16.650] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002167)

**[02:05:19.530] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002168)

**[02:05:29.500] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002169)  
Sir, kung nabawasan po talaga, however, pwede hong mag-supplemental budget po during that time na ma-fill upon na po yung position.

**[02:05:41.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002170)  
Sorry, what do you mean by supplemental budget?

**[02:05:44.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002171)

**[02:05:48.520] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002172)

**[02:05:52.860] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002173)  
the total budget request for those hospitals.

**[02:05:56.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002174)

**[02:06:07.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002175)  
So, ang concern natin ay kulang na nga tapos kayong tinanggalan pa kayo ng additional plantilla positions.

**[02:06:18.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002176)

**[02:06:25.160] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002177)  
Sir, pwede pa po.

**[02:06:26.220] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002178)

**[02:06:30.540] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002179)  
For personal services.

**[02:06:32.720] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002180)  
Only, again, the difference is explained by the fact that these have been unfilled positions.

**[02:06:39.040] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002181)  
But the total budget po for personal services would remain the same.

**[02:06:47.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002182)  
Mr. Chen, naubos yung oras dito.

**[02:06:49.210] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002183)

**[02:06:56.930] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002184)

**[02:06:58.270] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002185)

**[02:07:00.850] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002186)

**[02:07:02.790] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002187)  
Ano bang problema?

**[02:07:04.050] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002188)

**[02:07:11.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002190)

**[02:07:15.690] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002191)

**[02:07:29.470] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002192)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:07:29.890] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002193)

**[02:07:32.230] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002194)  
Please.

**[02:07:32.790] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002195)

**[02:07:35.690] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002196)

**[02:07:37.010] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002197)

**[02:07:39.850] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002198)

**[02:07:43.150] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002199)

**[02:07:46.130] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002200)

**[02:07:47.530] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002201)  
Could we just briefly hear kung bakit.

**[02:07:48.950] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002202)  
Anong problema sa unfilled position?

**[02:07:50.990] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002203)  
Please reply.

**[02:07:55.050] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002204)  
Sir, yung mga unfilled positions po would be for various reasons.

**[02:07:59.750] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002205)  
Number one po, may mga positions na there are no takers.

**[02:08:04.110] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002206)  
Especially those medical positions which would have more competitive salaries elsewhere.

**[02:08:12.490] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002207)  
Number two, yung mga may ibang hospitals po na yung ibang positions are really for residencies and fellowships.

**[02:08:21.070] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002208)  
That's why they are not filled up permanently because these are temporary in nature.

**[02:08:27.770] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002209)  
Yun po, sir.

**[02:08:29.630] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002210)  
Yung ibang mga reasons for unfilled.

**[02:08:34.110] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002211)  
Posing up of positions.

**[02:08:35.610] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002212)

**[02:08:39.770] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002213)  
Sir, mga physical therapies, development, speech therapies, etc. po.

**[02:08:46.350] DOH Undersecretary Maylene M. Beltran** (asr-002214)  
Especially.

**[02:08:47.790] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002215)  
So, isama na po yun sa isasubmit.

**[02:08:50.810] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002216)  
Ano yung mga nature at anong problema?

**[02:08:52.950] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002217)

**[02:08:54.850] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002218)

**[02:08:57.570] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002219)  
Okay.

**[02:08:58.270] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002220)  
Mr. Chair, thank you.

**[02:09:05.150] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002227)  
Cut to MOE.

**[02:09:06.510] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002228)  
So, again, alam natin napakalala ng problema ng sitwasyon sa mga public hospitals.

**[02:09:13.950] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002229)

**[02:09:16.890] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002230)

**[02:09:24.430] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002231)

**[02:09:27.410] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002232)

**[02:09:33.510] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002233)  
Yung kanilang MOE, isang example lang yun, National Center for Mental Health, 311 million cut in MOE.

**[02:09:43.390] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002234)  
Please explain.

**[02:09:52.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002235)  
Habang merong policy ng zero balance billing, libre, babawasan naman yung MOE.

**[02:09:57.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002236)  
Paano yun?

**[02:10:05.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002237)  
Mr. Chair, ito po ay sa DOH Regional Hospitals, bukod pa po itong specialty hospitals.

**[02:10:12.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002238)  
Ikinukuha lang yan.

**[02:10:14.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002239)  
Doon po naman sa aming...

**[02:10:16.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002240)  
Dito po sa ating mga regional hospitals, ang aming GAA noong 2026 ay 101.7, ang NEPO ay 102.9.

**[02:10:28.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002241)  
So, yun po ay for regional hospitals ng DOH ay tumaas po ng 1.1%.

**[02:10:35.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002242)  
Pero yung operations of DOH hospitals in Metro Manila, yun po yung bumaba from 30 billion noong 2026 GAA to 26.9 billion,

**[02:10:46.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002243)  
which is lower than 10.2%.

**[02:10:48.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002244)  
Ah, anong reason? Yung, although ang hiningi po namin ay 31.7 po para nga po to cover our projected MOOE, pero nag-grant lang po ay 26.9.

**[02:11:05.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002244-split-2)  
So, bakit nga po, nakita ko, Batanes Provincial General Hospital, ang chairman ng health, galing sa

**[02:11:19.830] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002246)  
Batanes, binawasan din ang MOE. So, wala kayong explanation. Is it a DBM matter? Then, Chair, can we ask the DBM, yung representative dito ng DBM, bakit binawasan yung MOE ng mga ospital na ito? E gipit na gipit na nga. Is the representative of DBM present? May taga-DBM po ba na nandito ngayon? Ayun.

**[02:11:49.720] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002246-split-2)  
Quick interjection, Mr. Chair, if the honorable, my very good friend, Hon. Tinio will allow.

**[02:11:54.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002247)  
Please proceed.

**[02:11:56.900] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002247-split-2)  
Yes, thank you very much, Mr. Chair. In relation to the question of the Honorable Tinio, may I also propose that the Department of Health furnish the Committee on Appropriations a copy of the field health reimbursements of each hospital, as well as medical assistance program allocation of each hospital, including the actual utilization of their MOOE because this might be the reason

**[02:12:24.780] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002248)  
why there was a cut in their MOOE because there was a higher allocation of MAIFIP as well as PhilHealth reimbursements. I so move, Mr. Chair. And thank you very much, Congressman Tolosa.

**[02:12:39.920] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002248-split-2)  
Congressman Tinio!

**[02:12:41.760] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002248-split-3)  
Sorry, Mr. Chair. Congressman Tinio, my very good friend.

**[02:12:48.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002248-split-4)  
I believe that was a motion, DS. Yes. Are there any seconds?

**[02:12:54.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002249)  
I second that.

**[02:12:56.030] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002249-split-2)  
Sige. Okay. Pero gusto pa rin po natin marinig, ano yung justification for cutting the MOOE of these DOH hospitals?

**[02:13:03.090] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002249-split-3)  
So the documents are requested to be submitted para makita rin po natin.

**[02:13:12.750] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002249-split-3-split-2)  
Wala po ba yung DBM dito? Ah, nandiyan na ba? Alam ko meron.

**[02:13:17.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002249-split-3-split-2-split-2)  
Ayan, katabi niyo. Ah, please reply to the question of Congressman Tinio.

**[02:13:22.410] DBM Official** (asr-002249-split-4)  
Yes po, good morning po, Mr. Chair, Your Honor. Ah, actually po yung

**[02:13:26.470] DBM Official** (asr-002250)  
MOE naman po ng hospitals for DOH, we just follow naman po yung normative financing as provided din naman po by the DOH. And sila rin po yung nagbe-break ng MOE requirements. So, there may have, meron pong mga nag-decrease nga po from the 2026. But this is based naman po sa normative financing. And additionally, as mentioned nga din po ni Secretary kanina, a portion po, meron

**[02:13:56.520] DBM Official** (asr-002251)  
pong portion na nailagay sa PhilHealth to implement the CBB, implementation of the CBB, which will eventually rin naman po is papasok din sa mga, as additional MOE po ng mga hospitals. So, yun po yung naging policy for the 2027 budget na ang tingin po kasi is sectoral nga po yung tingin natin ngayon, not just yung sa DOH lang, kasama po yung sa PhilHealth dahil nga po mayroon nga po doon na dagdag na around 14 billion to implement din po yung CBB po ng DOH. Thank you po.

**[02:14:35.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002252)  
Could we have a response from the DOH Secretary on that? Parang nagtuturuan. Una, sabi nila, well, binatay lang namin sa sinubmit niyo. Pangalawa, the other one is binawasan yung MOE dahil mababawi naman sa PhilHealth through the ZBB, zero balance billing. Could we have a response?

**[02:15:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002259)  
Di ba may problema doon?

**[02:15:02.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002260)  
Please reply, Sec. Mercado.

**[02:15:05.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002261)  
So, all the OH hospitals are allowed income retention.

**[02:15:09.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002262)  
So, as mentioned earlier by Kong Garin, we should look at it in totality.

**[02:15:16.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002263)  
Kung makano po yung iba pang revenue from PhilHealth from Ma'a-Ethip.

**[02:15:22.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002264)  
Kasi nga po, ang understanding ko nung kami po ay nag-usap ni Sec. Kim,

**[02:15:26.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002265)

**[02:15:30.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002266)

**[02:15:37.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002267)  
Kaya po, gaya po na nasabi ng ating representative from DBM,

**[02:15:43.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002268)  
it's a sector approach na parang in a way itong kabuan,

**[02:15:50.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002269)

**[02:15:55.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002270)  
Baka yun po yung kailangang kuntingnan din at bibigay po nga po namin yung ating datos dito po as requested po.

**[02:16:05.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002271)  
Please continue.

**[02:16:06.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002272)  
Mr. Secretary, hindi po ba problema to na binabawasan na yung MOE?

**[02:16:13.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002273)

**[02:16:18.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002274)

**[02:16:23.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002275)

**[02:16:25.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002276)

**[02:16:29.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002277)

**[02:16:30.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002278)

**[02:16:35.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002279)

**[02:16:42.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002280)

**[02:16:47.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002281)  
Hindi ba problema yun?

**[02:16:49.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002282)

**[02:16:52.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002283)  
Pero ang ating nga pong next step,

**[02:16:55.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002284)  
progress po tayo doon sa tinatawag na global payment.

**[02:16:58.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002285)
