**[02:17:05.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002286)  
At ifo, front load na po ng PhilHealth yun sa ating mga hospital.

**[02:17:11.290] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002287)  
Okay, malinaw, sir.

**[02:17:12.150] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002288)  
Ang kailangan, yung pera, direct ang nasa hospital para direct ang mabigay yung servisyo.

**[02:17:19.570] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002289)  
Hindi yung...

**[02:17:20.830] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002290)  
Usually, anong sinasabi ng mga hospital?

**[02:17:23.530] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002291)  
Sorry, wala kami yan. Bumili kayo sa labas.

**[02:17:26.070] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002292)  
Di ba, ganun ang nangyayari?

**[02:17:27.630] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002293)  
O, dahil kulang ang budget nila.

**[02:17:32.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002294)  
So, chair, again, at tinututulan po natin yung ganitong kalakaran na mukhang pinagkakasunduan ng DBM at DOH ngayon na

**[02:17:43.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002295)  
dahil meron naman daw MyFIP, meron namang PhilHealth, bawasan na natin yung MOOE ng mga hospital.

**[02:17:53.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002296)  
Kailangan po, tiyaki, nandyan ready ang pondo.

**[02:17:57.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002297)  
My last question.

**[02:17:59.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002298)  
Mr. Chair.

**[02:18:00.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002299)  
Please proceed.

**[02:18:00.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002300)  
Opo.

**[02:18:01.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002301)  
Ay, ano po ang...

**[02:18:03.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002302)  
I mean, malinaw po na kulang ang ating capacity.

**[02:18:07.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002303)  
In the end, bed capacity.

**[02:18:08.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002304)  
You mentioned DOH hospitals have, was it 23,000 total bed capacity?

**[02:18:14.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002305)  
Pero, ang karanasan ng mga kababayan natin, talagang punong-puno.

**[02:18:20.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002306)  
Lalo pa, pag may mga surge during leptospirosis or dengue and so on, punong-puno ang mga hospital, ang mga ward.

**[02:18:28.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002307)  
Mga emergency room, ginagawang ward.

**[02:18:31.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002308)  
Ano po ang ginagawa nyo at nasaan sa budget na ito, yung programa para sa expansion of bed capacity ng mga DOH hospitals?

**[02:18:45.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002309)  
Ano yung achievement ng administrasyong ito?

**[02:18:49.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002310)  
How many beds have been added to the DOH hospitals by this administration?

**[02:18:55.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002311)  
At, for the...

**[02:18:58.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002312)  
For the proposed year, next year, ilan ang dadagdag pa na bed capacity?

**[02:19:06.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002313)  
At, meron ba tayong plano in the first place para dito?

**[02:19:11.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002314)  
Mr. Chair, yung akin pong immediate na pwedeng gawin, kasi may lead time din po yung construction, is really efficiencies.

**[02:19:20.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002315)  
Ngayon, ang nasabi ko, kung ma-reduce po natin yung average length of stay, dahil mas maganda po yung treatment,

**[02:19:25.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002316)  
ay in effect, kung ano po yung reduction dun sa tagal.

**[02:19:30.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002317)  
Kung ano po na tumatagal yung ating pasyente sa hospital, ay parang katumbas na rin ang karagdagang beds po yun.

**[02:19:36.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002318)  
Pero, ang isa pa rin po namin ginagawa sa tulong po ng PhilHealth, ay magkaroon din po ng parang inter-referral system.

**[02:19:44.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002319)  
Yun naman po yung nakasaad dito sa ating UHC law, kung saan din po magtatap na tayo dun sa local government unit hospitals at saka mga private hospitals.

**[02:19:53.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002320)  
So, meron na po tayong talagang tinatakda na number of beds.

**[02:19:58.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002321)  
At, meron na po mga...

**[02:20:00.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002322)  
ng hospital na nag-land 10% of their beds.

**[02:20:02.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002323)  
Na pagka meron pong apaw dito sa ating, in spite of all the efficiencies, ay pwede rin na po natin gamitin yung mga NBB beds na mga private hospital.

**[02:20:13.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002324)  
Although, yun po ay mga short, ang matagalan po talaga, i-expand po yung actual physical plant ng mga DOH hospitals.

**[02:20:23.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002325)  
So, thank you.

**[02:20:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002326)  
Although, in short, Mr. Chair, ang intindi ko lang sa sagot ay walang plano ang DOH.

**[02:20:30.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002327)  
Sa kagyat, and maybe until the end of the administration, walang sistematikong plano para ma-expand yung bed capacity ng mga DOH hospitals.

**[02:20:40.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002328)  
Yun ang dinig ko. Sabi, must focus on efficiencies and maybe distribute to the local hospitals and so on.

**[02:20:50.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002329)  
So, well, we will take this up further. Tingin natin may problema yan.

**[02:20:55.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002330)  
No? Dahil, yun ang pangunahing...

**[02:21:00.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002331)  
Maganda, merong zero balance billing program, pero kung limitado ang kapasidad, ay limitado ang makikinabang na mamamayan natin,

**[02:21:11.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002332)  
at inevitably, ipapasa sila sa private sector na mas mahal.

**[02:21:16.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002333)  
Follow up ko na lang po, please submit a report on this administration's achievements in expanding capacity and any plans, kung meron man,

**[02:21:29.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002334)  
in that program.

**[02:21:30.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002335)  
With that regard, thank you po.

**[02:21:31.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002336)  
Yes, maraming salamat.

**[02:21:33.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002337)  
Can I do that, Mr. Jointer?

**[02:21:34.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002338)  
Yes, last reply ni Secretary.

**[02:21:35.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002339)  
Nakuha ko na po yung datos.

**[02:21:36.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002340)  
Ang amin pong estimate, yung kasalukuyan pong bed capacity ay 30,000, ay aakyat po ng 45,000 by 2028.

**[02:21:46.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002341)  
So, meron naman pong program. Isasama na lang po namin.

**[02:21:48.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002342)  
Sir, pakiulit lang po. By 2028?

**[02:21:52.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002343)  
Current po na authorized bed capacity po, ngayon, noong 2025 na 30,000, ay aakyat po ng 45,000.

**[02:22:00.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002344)  
So, in two years, ay dadagdagan niyo by 15,000 yung bed capacity ng mga DOH hospitals.

**[02:22:12.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002345)  
That's an important statement.

**[02:22:15.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002346)  
Please clarify, sir, para mas maintindihan po ng committee.

**[02:22:20.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002347)  
At parang reflected ba sa budget yan? You have only until 27-28.

**[02:22:26.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002348)  
Naka-reflect naman po dun sa budget ng expansion.

**[02:22:33.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002349)  
At yun nga lang po, dahil nag-cut dun sa HVEP, medyo maka-ma-delay ng konti dito sa target.

**[02:22:44.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002350)  
Unless nga po ma-reinstate natin yung ating pong hinihingi ng allocation.

**[02:22:49.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002351)  
Mr. Chair?

**[02:22:50.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002352)  
Tin-report na lang, Chair. Thank you.

**[02:22:51.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002353)  
So, sek, paki yung comprehensive answer sa mga questions ni DML Tino.

**[02:23:00.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002354)  
At yung motion ni DS Garibaldi.

**[02:23:03.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002355)  
Pa rin, pakisagot po in written form para mas ma-appreciate ng committee and its members.

**[02:23:11.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002356)  
Nang sa ganun, ay mas mapaliwanag natin ng mas maigi.

**[02:23:14.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002357)  
And I understand yung efficiencies na sinasabi siguro ni Secretary.

**[02:23:20.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002358)  
Yung bylaw, kailangan pati yung private hospital mag-create ng 10% ward.

**[02:23:26.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002359)  
Kung magagawa ito, that will expand the bed capacity.

**[02:23:31.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002360)  
Not only of the government hospital, pero magagamit natin yung existing facilities din ng private hospital.

**[02:23:39.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002361)  
So, malaking bagay yung paki-explain din po sa inyong written answers.

**[02:23:46.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002362)  
So, thank you very much, DML Tino, for your questions and queries.

**[02:23:52.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002363)  
Rest assured, ipapasa po natin yung mga sagot ng DOH and PhilHealth sa committee at sa inyo po.

**[02:23:59.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002364)  
Okay, so.

**[02:24:01.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002365)  
Next, we go back to the majority.

**[02:24:05.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002366)  
May we call on our Deputy Speaker, Ferge Biron, for his questions.

**[02:24:11.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002367)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:24:13.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002368)  
First of all, let me congratulate Secretary Mercado and Dr. Ho for your respective appointments.

**[02:24:21.820] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002369)  
So, while I was observing the interpellation of Congressman Tino,

**[02:24:28.720] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002370)  
I observed that the good Secretary,

**[02:24:31.500] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002371)  
he has some difficulty struggling on issues pertaining to the budget of the DOH.

**[02:24:41.400] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002372)  
May I know from the Secretary,

**[02:24:43.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002373)  
if this budget is your own doing or is a budget formulated by your predecessor?

**[02:24:53.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002374)  
When I came, Mr. Chairman, I came in po na meron na pong approval yung NEP.

**[02:24:57.910] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002375)  
So, it's already, kumbaga, the budget from, yes.

**[02:25:03.170] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002376)  
Yes, very obvious naman siya eh, na hindi niyo budget ito eh.

**[02:25:07.990] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002377)  
So, I would suggest, this representation would like to suggest to the Secretary,

**[02:25:13.490] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002378)  
you have to be honest and candid to Congress.

**[02:25:17.410] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002379)  
Kung meron kang nakita na hindi mo gusto,

**[02:25:20.730] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002380)  
or gusto mo i-amendahan, or gusto mo dagdagan,

**[02:25:24.170] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002381)  
sabihin mo sa amin, because we're here to help you and help the Department.

**[02:25:27.970] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002382)  
So, my first question, Mr. Secretary, is,

**[02:25:32.030] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002383)  
10 years ago,

**[02:25:33.170] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002384)  
the bed-to-patient ratio in the hospital for both government and private hospital is 0.8

**[02:25:41.950] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002385)  
to 1,000 population.

**[02:25:47.800] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002386)  
Meaning to say, we have a deficit of around 140,000 beds.

**[02:25:53.620] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002387)  
That was 10 years ago.

**[02:25:56.200] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002388)  
So, may I know from the Secretary, what is the present bed-to-patient ratio?

**[02:26:02.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002389)  
In Metro Manila po, it's 1.7.

**[02:26:08.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002390)  
So, yun po ay almost compliant to the WHO standard of 3.

**[02:26:15.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002391)  
Overall, nationwide, dahil nga po mas konti yung bed-to-patient ratio sa ating probinsya,

**[02:26:22.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002392)  
bababa po ng mga 1.1, 1.2.

**[02:26:27.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002393)  
So, we're still much under the prescribed bed-to-patient ratio provided by WHO.

**[02:26:36.980] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002394)  
Okay, moving on to the...

**[02:26:38.100] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002395)  
Next question, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:26:42.180] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002396)  
I'm sure you're aware that the global benchmark for national expenditure on health is 10% of GDP.

**[02:26:51.200] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002397)  
Do you agree with me?

**[02:26:53.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002398)  
Yes po, yun po yung standard benchmark.

**[02:26:56.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002399)  
Yes.

**[02:26:57.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002400)  
But for developing economies like the Philippines,

**[02:27:00.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002401)  
acceptable na po yung 4 to 5%.

**[02:27:03.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002402)  
So, given your budget of P353 billion,

**[02:27:06.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002403)  
that is all...

**[02:27:07.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002404)  
Almost 4.5% of the national budget.

**[02:27:13.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002405)  
Although, when you consider po yung percentage of GDP,

**[02:27:17.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002406)  
it's a combination po of government and private spending.

**[02:27:21.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002407)  
So, government right now accounts for 46% of the total healthcare spend.

**[02:27:27.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002408)  
Roughly half.

**[02:27:29.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002409)  
So, times 2 po, baka abot na po ng 7% kung kasama po yung spending ng mga...

**[02:27:34.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002410)  
So, aabot tayo ng 7% of GDP.

**[02:27:37.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002411)  
Which is quite acceptable.

**[02:27:40.380] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002412)  
So, given the scarce resources of the government,

**[02:27:44.600] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002413)  
your department is beset with a serious problem on expired medicines.

**[02:27:51.580] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002414)  
Maliit na nga yung ating budget, maliit ang pera natin.

**[02:27:55.040] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002415)  
Pero marami pa rin ang problema ng DOH sa expired medicines.

**[02:28:00.020] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002416)  
Can you provide this committee with a report of expired medicines since 2022?

**[02:28:07.220] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002417)  
Year on year until today.

**[02:28:12.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002418)  
We will provide po the data, Mr. Chair.

**[02:28:16.670] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002419)  
Kung year on year po, starting 2022.

**[02:28:20.030] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002420)  
Okay. So, Mr. Chair, I would like to request the DOH to furnish this committee and this representation a copy of those reports.

**[02:28:31.430] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002421)  
But while waiting for those reports, may I know from the good secretary, what are the reasons, where do you attribute...

**[02:28:39.970] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002422)  
this problem on expired medicines?

**[02:28:42.730] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002423)  
Kasi, this accounts for hundreds of millions since 2022.

**[02:28:48.630] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002424)  
Ang tanong is, is it attributed to poor procurement planning, inventory management control?

**[02:28:56.610] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002425)  
Where do you think is the problem?

**[02:28:58.050] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002426)  
From my initial analysis po, given the three weeks that I was on the job,

**[02:29:06.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002427)  
wala hong automated na talagang supply chain system.

**[02:29:11.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002428)  
That already tracks stock level down to the level of the CHDs.

**[02:29:15.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002429)  
It's all pen and paper, which we need to really improve on.

**[02:29:21.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002430)  
But the other thing that we're looking at, given nga po na PhilHealth is expanding its coverage of gamot,

**[02:29:27.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002431)  
some of the commodities can also be procured through the gamot program of PhilHealth.

**[02:29:33.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002432)  
Para rin wala pong double financing.

**[02:29:37.460] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002433)  
You mean to say, Mr. Secretary, hanggang ngayon walang operating software?

**[02:29:41.260] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002434)  
Ang warehouse ninyo?

**[02:29:43.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002435)  
Meron pong LMEIS, ito po ay donated, pero hindi po ganong kaganda ang feedback po sa atin.

**[02:29:53.200] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002436)  
So, all of these medicines procured in the central office get stocked in your central warehouse?

**[02:30:00.080] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002437)  
At tumatagal doon at hindi na dideliver sa central, sa CHDs, regional offices.

**[02:30:11.450] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002438)  
So ang ginawa nga po ng ating bagong procurement undersecretary ay naggawa po ng sistema na bago po mag-procure o mag-bid out ng bagong mga commodities,

**[02:30:24.090] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002439)  
ay inaalam po yung mga stock level doon sa iba't ibang region, kinoconsolidate.

**[02:30:28.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002440)  
At pagsatansya po namin ay may sapat pa, hindi po muna gagawin yung procurement.

**[02:30:33.510] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002441)  
That's one. Number two po ay yun nga, meron tayong electronic inventory system na kailangan po natin i-upgrade.

**[02:30:45.990] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002442)  
How much do you spend? How much does DOH spend on warehousing? What is the budget on warehousing and logistics annually, Mr. Secretary?

**[02:31:03.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002443)  
Ang supply chain management including warehousing and tracking po, last year was 1.2 billion.

**[02:31:11.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002444)  
And for this year, approved NEP is 1.23. May konti pong increase ng 1.8%.

**[02:31:18.980] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002445)  
If you itemize the budget, how much goes to warehousing? How much goes to logistics?

**[02:31:25.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002446)  
Out of the 1.2 billion, give or take po, 250 or almost 1.4 or 1.5 is in warehousing, 15 million for electronics.

**[02:31:47.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002447)  
And 90 million po for operating expense.

**[02:31:54.700] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002448)  
Given this huge budget for warehousing and logistics, does it make sense that most of your national program should be downloaded to the regions?

**[02:32:08.080] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002449)  
Regionalize ninyo ang mga national program kasi nangyayari is, pinoprocure ng central, hindi naman nadideliver sa region.

**[02:32:15.660] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002450)  
Katulad ng TB program ninyo.

**[02:32:17.660] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002451)  
HIV program ninyo.

**[02:32:19.120] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002452)  
Family planning program ninyo.

**[02:32:21.180] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002453)  
They all get stuck in the central warehouse without it being delivered to the regional offices.

**[02:32:26.560] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002454)  
Would you agree with me that it's now the time to regionalize procurement?

**[02:32:31.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002455)  
Yes, sir. In fact, we already are regionalizing. 60% of our commodities are actually procured at the region.

**[02:32:38.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002456)  
These are the more commonly available meds.

**[02:32:41.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002457)  
Ang naka-centralized lang po talaga are the high-value medicines like cancer meds and vaccine.

**[02:32:47.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002458)  
Immunizations po.

**[02:32:49.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002459)  
So 60% naman po nasa region.

**[02:32:51.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002460)  
Yung nabanggit ko pong warehousing, kasama din po doon ang regions.

**[02:32:56.480] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002461)  
So the cost of warehousing includes the other regions?

**[02:33:00.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002462)  
Includes na po the other regions.

**[02:33:02.840] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002463)  
Well, I will have other questions, Mr. Secretary, with respect to HVEP.

**[02:33:07.360] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002464)  
But let me move on to FDA.

**[02:33:10.740] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002465)  
Is DG Tanakin around?

**[02:33:12.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002466)  
May I ask the good Director General to respond to some questions?

**[02:33:26.880] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002467)  
DG, may problema ang FDA, no?

**[02:33:31.260] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002468)  
It's been a problem on registrations.

**[02:33:37.320] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002469)  
And I believe the only way to address this is to fully digitalize the FDA,

**[02:33:43.320] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002470)  
especially the CDRR and the Medical Device Division.

**[02:33:46.820] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002471)  
Can you elaborate on the budget that you want Congress to provide you?

**[02:33:53.000] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002472)  
Thank you, Congressman Beron.

**[02:33:56.940] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002473)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:33:59.120] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002474)  
The FDA has always been complaining of the lack of personnel

**[02:34:05.240] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002475)  
and the influx of the very high volume of applications.

**[02:34:12.560] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002476)  
So what the CDRR thought of doing in collaboration with our other centers

**[02:34:17.540] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002477)  
is to come up with a system, a digital system,

**[02:34:24.960] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002478)  
whereby the applications will...

**[02:34:29.120] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002479)  
whereby the applications will be processed or evaluated.

**[02:34:33.900] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002480)  
So basically, it's an end-to-end digital system, sir.

**[02:34:38.120] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002481)  
In terms of the budget, it will require about ₱217 million.

**[02:34:42.620] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002482)  
That is the minimum estimate.

**[02:34:44.900] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002483)  
This is not a program, a subscription-based program, sir.

**[02:34:49.640] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002484)  
This will be an in-house program that is really designed for CDRR,

**[02:34:55.100] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002485)  
which can hopefully be...

**[02:34:59.120] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002486)  
also replicated by the other centers as well, sir.

**[02:35:02.840] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002487)  
What is the total budget that you are requesting Congress to provide you?

**[02:35:07.300] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002488)  
The total budget just for the system, sir, is ₱217 million approximately.

**[02:35:12.860] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002489)  
But the total MOOE that we would appeal, sir, is ₱517...

**[02:35:21.200] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002490)  
I'm sorry.

**[02:35:27.840] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002491)  
₱589 million.

**[02:35:30.260] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002492)  
Yeah, ₱518 is the one that will be provided by Congress.

**[02:35:33.400] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002493)  
But what is the expected revenue from the retained income of FDA?

**[02:35:40.240] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002494)  
What again, sir?

**[02:35:42.180] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002495)  
₱518 million is the one that will be provided by Congress in the GAA.

**[02:35:46.180] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002496)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:35:46.680] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002497)  
So, but I'm sure that you have retained income

**[02:35:49.540] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002498)  
coming from the revenues generated by the agency.

**[02:35:52.980] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002499)  
How much is the counterpart of the revenue provided by the retention of your income?

**[02:35:59.920] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002500)  
Based on our current record, sir,

**[02:36:01.860] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002501)  
it's amounting to approximately...

**[02:36:03.400] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002502)  
Approximately ₱2 billion, Your Honor.

**[02:36:07.020] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002503)  
But under current system, sir,

**[02:36:10.280] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002504)  
we don't have access to that retained income

**[02:36:13.820] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002505)  
unless it's time that we are allowed to touch.

**[02:36:19.240] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002506)  
That we are hoping that the ₱580 million will be allowed to get from that SAGF, sir.

**[02:36:28.340] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002507)  
Okay.

**[02:36:28.920] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002508)  
So, one more last question, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:36:33.400] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002509)  
So, your budget that you're asking Congress is from the date 18.

**[02:36:38.120] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002510)  
Presently, you are not in the position to tap the retained income

**[02:36:41.880] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002511)  
unless you submit to DBM your business plan.

**[02:36:45.640] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002512)  
Is that correct?

**[02:36:46.940] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002513)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:36:47.920] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002514)  
But we have already, the previous DG, DG Teston,

**[02:36:51.860] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002515)  
has already submitted the five-year development plan,

**[02:36:54.720] FDA OIC Director General Atty. Franklin Anthony M. Tabaquin IV** (asr-002516)  
which is one of the conditions for us to be able to...

**[02:36:57.820] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002517)  
Okay, thank you, DG.

**[02:36:58.720] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002518)  
Mr. Chair, I will reserve my other questions for Phil Wealth

**[02:37:02.520] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002519)  
in the second round.

**[02:37:03.720] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002520)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[02:37:04.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002521)  
Maraming salamat, Deputy Speaker Ferge at Attorney Tabakin.

**[02:37:09.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002522)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:37:10.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002523)  
And DOH is requested to submit the data requested by DS Ferge,

**[02:37:17.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002524)  
especially those on the expired medicine.

**[02:37:21.620] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002525)  
Mr. Chair, if the Honorable Biron is willing,

**[02:37:25.980] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002526)  
may I amend his motion that the list include not only expired,

**[02:37:29.940] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002527)  
but also expiring and near-expiring medicines.

**[02:37:32.520] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-002528)  
So, moved, Mr. Chair.

**[02:37:35.300] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002529)  
Second, Mr. Chair.

**[02:37:36.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002530)  
There is a motion.

**[02:37:37.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002531)  
Julie seconded.

**[02:37:39.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002532)  
The motion is approved.

**[02:37:41.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002533)  
Please, DOH, pakisubmit po yung expired, expiring, and near-expiring.

**[02:37:47.040] Deputy Speaker Ferjenel G. Biron, M.D.** (asr-002534)  
Mr. Chair, that includes the entire regional offices as well.

**[02:37:51.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002535)  
Okay, hanggang sa regional offices.

**[02:37:53.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002536)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[02:37:55.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002537)  
So, time check lang po.

**[02:37:58.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002538)  
It's 12.09.

**[02:38:00.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002539)  
So, I guess mamayang konti.

**[02:38:02.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002540)  
Magkaroon lang tayo ng biobreak.

**[02:38:06.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002541)  
And siguro working lunch na lang, if okay sa ating mga members.

**[02:38:11.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002542)  
So, for now, we recognize Assistant Minority Leader, Sarah Elago.

**[02:38:21.730] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002543)  
Please proceed, ma'am.

**[02:38:24.430] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002544)  
Mr. Chair, tungkol sa existing policies na kaugnay ng anti-hospital detention,

**[02:38:30.070] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002545)  
may we know if the DOH has the numbers regarding the unsettled promissory notes,

**[02:38:35.690] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002546)  
and the amount of collectibles from patients,

**[02:38:38.670] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002547)  
lumalaki po ba ito over the years?

**[02:38:59.030] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002548)  
Mr. Chair, if you can just provide the detailed data, unfortunately.

**[02:39:03.510] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002549)  
If it's okay with Congresswoman Sarah, okay lang po ba?

**[02:39:07.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002550)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[02:39:09.410] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002551)  
Now, does the department have an active monitoring of the magnitude of foreclosed or ceased collateral

**[02:39:17.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002552)  
because of these unsettled promissory notes?

**[02:39:20.890] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002553)  
Hingin po natin yung detalya, pero naistatis.

**[02:39:22.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002554)  
Hindi natin malaman.

**[02:39:23.670] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002555)  
Paano ba yung monitoring?

**[02:39:25.310] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002556)  
Paano natin natitiyak na kung may mga issues at concerns tungkol dito,

**[02:39:30.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002557)  
ay nasasagot natin agad?

**[02:39:34.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002558)  
So, as I mentioned earlier po, meron tayong parang action center, 1555.

**[02:39:41.550] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002559)  
Pero parang gusto nga naming palawigin,

**[02:39:44.550] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002560)  
and together with Phil Health, really manage it professionally.

**[02:39:49.330] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002561)  
So, yun po ay dinudulog doon, and then we have filled offices po to invest.

**[02:39:54.410] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002562)  
Investigate.

**[02:39:55.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002563)  
Because we also want to give due process to any complaints.

**[02:39:59.750] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002564)  
Mr. Chair, anong ibig sabihin for this action center to be managed by DOH professionally?

**[02:40:05.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002565)  
Ibig sabihin, is this service outsourced?

**[02:40:08.090] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002566)  
Or paano po ang management, administration, staffing, and funding for this particular action center?

**[02:40:16.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002567)  
It's internally managed po, and limited pa lang yung seats.

**[02:40:21.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002568)  
Kaya nga po, my experience when I was in Phil Health, it's an outsourced service.

**[02:40:26.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002569)  
And we get good statistics and reports, drop calls, how many are resolved, what are the most common complaints.

**[02:40:35.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002570)  
And then there are escalation also on kailan iyaakyat, can it be resolved at the level of the call center agent,

**[02:40:42.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002571)  
when it will be brought up to the level of the regional directors, and even all the way up to central office.

**[02:40:48.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002572)  
So, yun po yung ating pag-aaral.

**[02:40:50.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002573)  
Mr. Chair, kunin natin yung detalye ng mga naitawag na ilapit ng mga concerns dito sa action center.

**[02:40:56.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002574)  
Kung ano yung nature ng mga concerns ng mga tumatawag.

**[02:41:00.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002575)  
Natanong ko rin ito dahil maraming ospital, lalo na sa private hospitals na sapin-sapin ang dinaranas na pananamantala at hinagpis ng mga pasyente

**[02:41:09.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002576)  
dahil sa mga kondisyon sa pagbabayad ng kanilang balanse sa hospital bills.

**[02:41:14.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002577)  
At tulad ng pasyente, paano po kung walang pwedeng maikolateral na lupa o bahay yung mga pasyente, saan po sila maaaring lumapit sa ganitong mga kondisyon?

**[02:41:24.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002578)  
At pinapayagan.

**[02:41:26.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002579)  
At pinapayagan ba ng DOH itong pagpapataw ng mga pagkataas-taas po na compound interest rate sa mga kolateral?

**[02:41:46.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002580)  
Yung compounding po, wala pa pong policy.

**[02:41:50.680] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002581)  
Pero yung pong physical detention, yun po yung maliitong set rules.

**[02:41:57.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002582)  
Kasi parang commercial terms po yun that is entered into between the patient and the hospital.

**[02:42:03.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002583)  
Sir Chair, may batas din po tayo na anti-hospital detention.

**[02:42:10.330] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002584)  
At kinakailangan talaga ma-review na yung implementation na ito.

**[02:42:14.910] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002585)  
Because while it is true that RA 9439 allows these kinds of arrangements, it is an injustice to let things remain the same.

**[02:42:24.770] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002586)  
Sa halit po nabalansihin yung interest ng tubo sa mga ospital at na mga pasyente,

**[02:42:29.930] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002587)  
ang dapat po natin makonsidera ay ang paglalaan ng higit pang pondo sa mga pampublikong ospital

**[02:42:35.370] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002588)  
na sa gayon maging sapat ang mga facilities, ang kanilang bed capacity,

**[02:42:39.630] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002589)  
para sa mas marami nating mga nangangailangan ng mga kababayan.

**[02:42:44.590] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002590)  
At nagiging problema din po ito pag privatize talaga yung healthcare in general,

**[02:42:49.330] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002591)  
wherein we hide under the cloak of balancing interest while profiteering from supposed social services are actually left unchecked.

**[02:42:57.610] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002592)  
Kaugnay nito, Mr. Chair, lahat po ng mga lumapit din sa atin ng mga kaso,

**[02:43:01.930] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002593)  
kaugnay nito ay isusumitin natin sa DOH.

**[02:43:05.210] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002594)  
Sunod naman po, nais kong magtanong,

**[02:43:09.630] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002595)  
ang aditong nakalaan para sa Family Planning and Reproductive Health ay P664 million for 2020-7.

**[02:43:32.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002596)  
Mr. Chair, kaugnay nito, pwedeng makuha natin yung breakdown nitong P9.1 billion para sa Family Health, Immunization, Nutrition, and Responsible Parenting.

**[02:43:44.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002597)  
Nabanggit na po yung para sa RH.

**[02:43:47.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002598)  
Nabanggit na po yung ibang sub-programs sa ilalim nito.

**[02:43:50.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002599)  
We can provide po.

**[02:43:52.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002600)  
There's a list po on commodities, on women and children's protection, on safe motherhood,

**[02:43:59.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002601)  
on adolescent health program, on family planning program support.

**[02:44:03.100] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002602)  
We can give po the breakdown.

**[02:44:06.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002603)  
Mr. Chair, ahingin po natin yung detalye ng proposal, kaugnay nyan.

**[02:44:10.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002604)  
Pagdating naman sa women and children protection units,

**[02:44:14.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002605)  
magkano yung gagastusin natin?

**[02:44:17.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002606)  
At may plano ba na magsagawa, mag-establish pa ng mas marami nito sa buong bansa?

**[02:44:24.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002607)  
So, meron po tayong nakalaan na P51 million for women and children's protection support to women and children's protection unit in P51 hospitals po.

**[02:44:37.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002608)  
Sa P51 hospitals, may plano po tayo for next year na funding for establishment of new ones?

**[02:44:42.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002609)  
Pano magkano exista?

**[02:44:48.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002610)  
Yung P51, Mr. Chair?

**[02:44:50.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002611)  
Sir, is inclusive already of the expansion.

**[02:44:53.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-002612)  
I just, I'm asking how much is the current number?

**[02:44:59.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002613)  
Mr. Chair, kasama

**[02:45:00.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002614)  
rin ba sa P51M yung pagpupondo sa development at distribution ng Sexual Assault Investigation Kit na sariling gawa po ng mga Pilipino, yan ho ay isa sa mga tagumpay ng mga DOST-backed projects natin para magkaroon ng science-backed support services para sa access to justice ng mga nabibiktima ng abuso.

**[02:45:27.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002615)  
Yung P51M po ay kasama na yung kits although medyo we need data nga to see if it is sufficient kasi nga baka dun pa lang sa support ng program.

**[02:45:45.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002616)  
We will provide po makano po yung actual breakdown po nitong P51M for Women and Child Protection to include po kung makano yung kits.

**[02:45:55.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002617)  
At the other ones are to support the operation.

**[02:45:59.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002618)  
Yung kits po ay kukunin natin yung na-develop ng DOST. O meron po kayong ibang source?

**[02:46:17.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002619)  
Hindi pa po explicit Mr. Chair na yung DOST-developed kits ang ginagamit but we will review po kasi we can incorporate that in our TOR.

**[02:46:31.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002620)  
Yes, Mr. Chair. Napaka-ganda ko nung dinevelop mismo ng atin.

**[02:46:36.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002621)

**[02:46:45.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-002622)

**[02:56:53.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002748)

**[02:57:03.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002749)

**[02:57:07.210] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002751)

**[02:57:21.210] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002753)

**[02:57:42.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002754)

**[02:57:52.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002755)

**[02:57:56.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002756)

**[02:58:01.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002757)

**[02:58:19.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002759)

**[02:58:20.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002760)  
Thank you.

**[02:58:21.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002761)

**[02:58:28.720] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002762)

**[02:58:35.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002763)

**[02:58:47.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002764)

**[02:58:53.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002765)  
which PhilHealth and DOH combined can also at least do market signaling to be able to negotiate.

**[02:59:04.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002766)  
And given the current increase in gamot utilization plus the actual purchases of DOH,

**[02:59:12.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002767)  
that's a substantial volume to actually negotiate the price.

**[02:59:18.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002768)  
Also, we are looking at, again, the multiple drug.

**[02:59:24.920] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002770)  
Yung entry para may market friction, yung multiple players.

**[02:59:30.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-002771)  
For the FDA to also look into allowing multiple suppliers of certain essential drugs to create some market dynamics.

**[02:59:41.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002772)  
Thank you.

**[02:59:42.200] Rep. Rufus B. Rodriguez** (asr-002773)

**[03:58:20.580] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-002895)

Health Facility Development Bureau.

**[04:27:41.080] DOH Officer-in-Charge Assistant Secretary Dr. Melissa T. Sena** (asr-003550)

**[04:27:46.160] DOH Officer-in-Charge Assistant Secretary Dr. Melissa T. Sena** (asr-003551)

**[04:28:01.130] DOH Officer-in-Charge Assistant Secretary Dr. Melissa T. Sena** (asr-003553)

**[04:28:08.000] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003554)

**[04:28:16.160] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-003555)

**[04:30:20.720] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003571)  
May we ask Madam Chair if there's in any way that we can compel doctors to accept MAIFIP as a mode of payment for PF, Madam Chair?

**[04:30:30.460] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-003572)  
Secretary, would we be able to reply with regards to the policy on MAIFIP on professional fees?

**[04:30:43.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003573)  
Yes, Madam Chair, one of our amendments that we were proposing is to make it compulsory for doctors to accept.

**[04:30:52.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003574)  
It's part of the GAD and it should be implemented.

**[04:30:55.680] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003575)  
Amendment to what, Madam Chair, if I may ask?

**[04:31:02.670] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003576)  
Madam Chair, time's running.

**[04:31:06.670] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003577)  
It's a special provision, pardon me, it's not an amendment, but inclusion in the special provision of the GAA.

**[04:31:17.670] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003578)  
You will include it as a special amendment to the GAA?

**[04:31:23.870] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003579)  
Am I right, Madam Chair? Is there a way to compel doctors to accept PF?

**[04:31:35.070] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003580)  
We actually are starting to negotiate through the specialty societies.

**[04:31:42.010] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003581)  
But it's.

**[04:31:43.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003582)  
It's optional, that's why part of our, if you want to really compel, it becomes part of the GAA, but currently we're already in talks with certain specialty groups like the Philippine College of Surgeons, Philippine Obstetrics and Gynecological Society, PCP, to come up with a PF scheme that would either be a multiple of whatever PhilHealth is covering or base it on the RVU code of the HMO.

**[04:32:13.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003583)  
As basis for the PF.

**[04:32:15.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003584)  
So it's standardized.

**[04:32:17.510] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003585)  
But right now, we're still in talks.

**[04:32:20.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003586)  
So if we will make it optional, then it should be a pre-negotiated PF structure.

**[04:32:32.290] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003587)  
Okay, Madam Chair, because I've been asking since 19th Congress and it seems that I did not get any concrete answer on how we'll be able to compel doctors to accept my EP.

**[04:32:43.490] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003588)  
To pay for the PF, Madam Chair.

**[04:32:46.150] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003589)  
Hopefully, we will be, there will be a way that they'll be able to find it, to sign a memorandum of agreement or compel doctors to accept my EP as payment for PF, Madam Chair.

**[04:32:59.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003590)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[04:33:00.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003591)  
Actually, we are looking at packaging it together with the hospital.

**[04:33:04.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003592)  
We will come up with an opt-in mechanism.

**[04:33:07.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003593)

**[04:33:16.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003594)

**[04:33:20.820] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003595)

**[04:33:22.320] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003596)

**[04:33:26.120] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003598)

**[04:48:16.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003773)

**[04:48:18.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003774)  
Madam Chair, our rule po talaga dapat pag charity or basic accommodation dapat walang bayaran.

**[04:48:33.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003775)  
So yun po yung titingnan natin.

**[04:48:34.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003776)

**[04:48:41.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003777)

**[04:48:45.470] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003778)  
Ah,

**[04:48:47.030] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003779)  
Doon po sa basic accommodation, ang rule po naman na nakasaad doon sa UHC law ay dapat po no balance bill. Wala pong out-of-pocket na ilalabas.

**[04:48:57.890] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003780)

**[04:49:07.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003781)  
So yun nga po, ang tinitingnan namin, yung mga outliers na pwede maari pong falling between the cracks, na yun nga po dapat ang nako-cover naman ngayon ng second liner, which is the maipip.

**[04:49:23.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003782)

**[04:49:35.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-003783)

**[04:55:30.790] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003874)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:55:31.610] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003875)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:55:32.390] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003876)  
There are confluence of factors.

**[04:55:34.190] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003877)  
Number one, there was a protest by one of the disqualified bidders.

**[04:55:40.850] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003878)

**[04:55:45.190] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003879)  
So, it was endorsed to the predecessor of the acting secretary right now,

**[04:55:51.450] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003880)  
Secretary Puhalte.

**[04:55:52.330] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003881)  
And then, of course, he only remained with the DOH for, I think, two weeks.

**[04:55:59.590] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003882)  
So, and he was not able to resolve it because he's supposed to review the protest.

**[04:56:04.670] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003883)  
And then, acting Secretary Mercado already resolved the protest.

**[04:56:10.170] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003884)  
And we already...

**[04:56:13.170] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003885)

**[04:56:20.610] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003886)  
That is the one that has the protest, sir.

**[04:56:23.290] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003887)  
So, it was highly contentious.

**[04:56:25.250] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003888)  
But we already resolved, sir, that particular issue, sir.

**[04:56:30.210] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003889)  
Now, two reasons.

**[04:56:31.410] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003890)

**[04:56:32.370] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003891)  
Because of the protest, because of change in management.

**[04:56:35.510] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003892)  
Actually, yung protest, it depends on anong reason yung protest.

**[04:56:40.170] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003893)  
Because during a bidding process, napakadaling magdating.

**[04:56:43.290] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003894)  
It is qualified ng bidder.

**[04:56:45.270] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003895)

**[04:56:47.850] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003896)

**[04:56:50.110] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003897)

**[04:56:52.410] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003898)

**[04:56:57.050] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003899)

**[04:56:59.430] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003900)

**[04:57:03.090] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003901)

**[04:57:07.410] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003902)

**[04:57:10.250] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003903)

**[04:57:13.170] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003904)  
No, sir.

**[04:57:13.790] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003905)  
But, of course, the incoming secretary would have to do his due diligence regarding the matter.

**[04:57:19.870] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003906)

**[04:57:25.730] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003907)

**[04:57:28.250] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003908)

**[04:57:36.030] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003909)

**[04:57:37.170] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003910)

**[04:57:41.730] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003911)  
But, just the same, there has to be an independent evaluation by the Secretary of Health

**[04:57:47.770] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003912)  
in whether he agrees with the recommendation of the COVAX, sir.

**[04:57:51.390] DOH Undersecretary Christian Lel M. Saquilabon** (asr-003913)  
But, certainly, sir, moving forward, we'll abbreviate the process regarding procurement, sir.

**[04:57:58.790] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003914)

**[04:58:05.690] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003915)  
that's why there's a structure.

**[04:58:06.870] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003916)

**[04:58:11.730] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003917)

**[04:58:46.270] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003927)

**[05:09:29.950] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004120)

**[05:09:40.470] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004122)

**[05:09:45.070] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004124)

**[05:09:52.130] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004125)

**[05:10:03.470] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004127)

**[05:10:07.450] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004128)

**[05:10:10.290] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004129)

**[05:10:12.590] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004130)

**[05:10:16.970] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004131)

**[05:10:21.370] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004132)

**[05:10:24.750] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004133)

**[05:10:32.290] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004134)

**[05:10:38.050] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004135)

to the number of graduates every year

We actually have

**[05:14:06.480] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004199)

**[05:14:08.140] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004200)

**[05:14:11.120] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004201)  
We have the

**[05:14:12.300] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004202)

**[05:14:22.710] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004204)  
ang gusto ko lang malaman kung ano

**[05:14:25.250] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004205)  
ang plano natin para dumami

**[05:14:27.090] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004206)

**[05:14:29.950] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004208)

**[05:14:31.250] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004209)

**[05:14:34.070] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004210)  
So, baka we have to be

**[05:14:36.170] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004211)  
more creative and not just

**[05:14:38.070] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004212)

**[05:14:39.650] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004213)

**[05:14:41.950] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004214)  
programs to encourage more students

**[05:14:43.930] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004215)  
to get into medical school

**[05:14:45.770] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004216)  
and how to retain

**[05:14:47.290] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004217)  
our doctors to work in the country.

**[05:14:50.110] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004218)  
Maybe, I don't know.

**[05:14:51.990] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004219)  
You're the expert. So, ang tanong ko lang is

**[05:14:54.070] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004220)  
ano ang mga plano

**[05:14:56.050] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004221)  
natin for the next 10 years maybe?

**[05:14:59.150] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004222)  
Okay.

**[05:15:00.200] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004223)  
Yes.

**[05:15:00.750] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004224)  
Madam Chair, we do have scholarships. It is run by, DOH used to have what we call the pre-service scholarship programs. Nagpapaaral po tayo ng mga scholars sa medisina but that is already transitioning out dahil ang CHED po ang nagpapaaral na ngayon.

**[05:15:18.880] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004225)  
After the students graduate and they pass the licensure, we offer them jobs in the doctors to the barrio program that's under the National Health Workforce Support System.

**[05:15:37.940] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004226)  
We are actually expecting an increase in the number of doctors in the coming years because of the MSRS program.

**[05:15:53.300] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004227)  
Madam Chair, pero baka more importantly we have to open more government-owned medical schools kasi ang daming lumalapit gusto mag-medicine pero puno ang slots ng mga medical schools.

**[05:16:08.420] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004228)  
Maraming interested actually even without the scholarship programs. Ang problema kulang talaga ang mga eskwelahan. Hindi tayo pwede mag-depend sa CHED.

**[05:16:18.440] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004229)  
I think DOH, the healthcare system should push for more.

**[05:16:23.300] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004230)  
For more schools, not only for doctors but for allied professionals.

**[05:16:28.000] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004231)  
Yun lang po. So I hope we can do something about it.

**[05:16:32.140] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004232)  
Yun lang po. I just want to congratulate Dr. Mercado and Dr. Ho. I think DOH and PhilHealth are in good hands.

**[05:16:39.340] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004233)  
We are fully supportive of what you can do in the next few months.

**[05:16:43.740] Rep. Marlesa "Doc Marly" Hofer-Hasim** (asr-004234)  
I know medyo pag-upo nyo pa lang, budget hearing na pero kayang-kayang nyo po yan. We support both of you. Thank you.

**[05:16:51.300] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004235)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[05:16:53.300] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004236)  
Just to say po that DOH and CHED has actually entered into an agreement so that DOH is able to share with CHED the lack in terms of education, in terms of the professionals that we need in the health sector so that the production of our health workers is actually according to our needs.

**[05:17:21.160] DOH HHRDB Director IV Johanna Solon S. Banzon, RN, MScIH, DTMPH** (asr-004237)  
Maraming salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[05:17:23.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004238)  
Madam Chair, if I may add, with the progression of our universal healthcare and there are now HEPNs being established and there is income generated out of PhilHealth, out of the second line, MAIFIP, we're thinking that there will be more revenues coming in that can be used to incentivize healthcare workers.

**[05:17:45.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004239)  
And also in the long term because there is no more options in terms of career progression na hindi lang clinical yung focus.

**[05:17:53.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004240)  
But can even be a manager of a whole healthcare system.

**[05:17:57.160] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004241)  
I hope that would entice the healthcare workers to really stay on and look at the Philippines as a viable option long term.

**[05:18:09.860] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-004242)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[05:18:11.600] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-004243)  
Thank you, Cong Hofer-Hasim

**[05:18:13.840] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-004244)  
Next on deck is from the minority, Representative Renee Co.

**[05:18:17.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004245)  
Thank you very much, Madam Chair.

**[05:18:22.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004246)  
And good afternoon to the Department of Health.

**[05:18:24.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004247)  
And especially to its new leadership that in the past two months I assumed their term.

**[05:18:31.920] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004248)  
I'll go straight ahead na po.

**[05:18:34.720] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004249)  
Yung isang malaking issue ng kabataan at yung kalusugan nila ay yung kalusugang pangkaisipan or mental health.

**[05:18:43.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004250)  
At ang napansin naman po natin ay nagkaroon po ng budget cut sa pag-aalaga ng mental health.

**[05:18:51.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004251)  
Under the National Expenditure Program's allocation for the Department of Health.

**[05:18:58.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004252)  
In particular po, meron pong P311.49 million peso budget cut to MOOE ng NCMH.

**[05:19:07.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004253)  
That's 21%.

**[05:19:08.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004254)  
So given po na may estimated 12.5 million Filipinos that live with mental health conditions,

**[05:19:14.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004255)  
ano po ba yung rational and even ano po yung na-cut dito sa...

**[05:19:21.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004256)  
P311.49 million peso budget cut ng NCMH.

**[05:19:26.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004257)  
Madam Chair.

**[05:19:27.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004258)  
Madam Chair, this is along the line nga po ng pagbabawas po ng MOOE.

**[05:19:52.730] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004259)  
So ang MOOE po ng National Center for Mental Health nga po ay...

**[05:19:58.930] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004260)  
Madam Chair, may we request the resource person to speak closer to the mic.

**[05:20:05.310] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004261)  
Thank you.

**[05:20:08.070] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004262)  
So gaya po kanina na...

**[05:20:10.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004263)  
Napakita rin ni Representative Tino yung pagkakat po ng MOOE.

**[05:20:17.130] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004264)  
Ang sabi nga po namin, magbibigay kami ng report kung bakit po nag-cut.

**[05:20:22.290] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004265)  
Talaga po across the board, most of the DOH hospitals, MOOE budget was cut.

**[05:20:30.130] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004266)  
Kanina po over lunch, we were also talking.

**[05:20:32.510] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004267)  
Baka po kasi yung na-reallocate noong 2025.

**[05:20:37.110] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004268)  
From the DPWH to...

**[05:20:40.230] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004269)  
DOH, can be the reason.

**[05:20:43.090] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004270)  
Kaya umakyat po noong 2026.

**[05:20:46.030] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004271)  
Kaya nga po yung 2027, parang it's back to the usual normal.

**[05:20:50.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004272)  
So we will need to compare with 2023, 2024.

**[05:20:54.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004273)  
But we will provide po the exact data and the reason po.

**[05:20:58.290] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004274)  
Although ang pansin din naman po natin, even though may spike last year,

**[05:21:03.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004275)  
the National Mental Health Budget has been reduced by half every year since 2025.

**[05:21:09.030] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004276)  
From 1.5 billion...

**[05:21:10.230] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004277)  
to significantly smaller allocations.

**[05:21:13.610] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004278)  
And siguro ang tanong natin, dahil may push in legislation na magkaroon at mag-focus more on community-based mental health services.

**[05:21:23.330] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004279)  
And we see that downward trend.

**[05:21:25.110] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004280)  
Ano po ba yung mga mental health services or efforts na isentro sa community yung pag-aalaga dito na pinupondohan ngayon ng DOH?

**[05:21:40.230] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004281)  
And can I ask, may personal po ba na maaari pong tumutok dito?

**[05:21:46.050] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004282)  
Whether that is under PS or sa MOE, etc.

**[05:21:54.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004283)  
All the health workers, especially the nurses, are capacitated by our MHGAP trained staff.

**[05:22:04.490] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004284)  
And then we are also putting up our NCMH crisis hotline 24-7.

**[05:22:10.290] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004285)  
And then there will be a medical access program.

**[05:22:14.870] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004286)  
Across 1,296 sites.

**[05:22:18.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004287)  
Or mostly, almost 64% of the cities and municipalities.

**[05:22:23.290] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004288)  
And then PhilHealth nga po is also expanding its mental health program.

**[05:22:27.850] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004289)  
Kaya nga po yung aming interface is with settings-based impanelment,

**[05:22:35.310] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004290)  
mostly, especially in schools.

**[05:22:38.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004291)  
We will already coordinate with PhilHealth para po yung mental health program natin ma-augment.

**[05:22:44.870] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004292)  
Apart from what DOH is providing po.

**[05:22:48.190] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004293)  
May I ask po, Madam Chair, ilang years na po yung NCMH hotline?

**[05:22:52.130] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004294)  
At may napansin po ba tayong increase in the number of calls and the types of calls na trigger warning po,

**[05:22:59.750] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004295)  
tungkol po sa suicide in the past years?

**[05:23:05.730] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004296)  
Or this year?

**[05:23:07.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004297)  
So since 2019 po, ang cumulative call po natin ay 77,000.

**[05:23:20.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004298)  
At nakikita po natin yung...

**[05:23:22.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004299)  
Medyo trend from 3,000 noong 2019, 11,000 noong 2020,

**[05:23:28.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004300)  
21,000 po noong 2021, at 18,000 noong 2022.

**[05:23:34.220] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004301)  
Yun po yung kasagsagan ng COVID.

**[05:23:36.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004302)  
Tapos po, nag-continue po yung trend noong 2023, 16,000.

**[05:23:41.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004303)  
And noong 2024 po, 23,000.

**[05:23:44.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004304)  
So if you would look at it, talagang po merong trend of increasing calls dito sa ating hotlines po.

**[05:23:52.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004305)  
And may enough personnel po ba na-assign sa hotline or even yung magte-take on ng cases para po kung may mga natatanggap pong mga calls?

**[05:24:04.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004306)  
May 38 open lines po tayo.

**[05:24:08.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004307)  
Eight dedicated lines.

**[05:24:11.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004308)  
So nag-trunk search.

**[05:24:13.900] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-004309)  
At yun po ay month 24-7.

**[05:24:16.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004310)  
Dahil ang concern po natin, Madam Chair,

**[05:24:22.270] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004311)  
ay ang paulit-ulit po,

**[05:24:23.830] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004312)  
na statistic na kulang-nakulang po yung mental health professionals sa ating bansa.

**[05:24:29.830] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004313)  
So right now, yung number of licensed psychiatrists natin,

**[05:24:35.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004314)  
to my knowledge, it's less than 2,000.

**[05:24:39.330] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004315)  
And supposedly, sila ang mag-care of ng around 110 million Filipinos,

**[05:24:45.670] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004316)  
ma-ensure na kung ano man kailangan nila ay makakaprovide.

**[05:24:48.870] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004317)  
And may mga mismatches pa rin,

**[05:24:51.850] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004318)  
whether that's the supply,

**[05:24:53.830] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004319)  
of psychologists, psychometricians,

**[05:24:56.330] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004320)  
and even yung supplementary effort ngayon ng Department of Education na may guidance associates,

**[05:25:03.490] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004321)  
hindi pa rin po ma-meet yung pangangailangan na demand.

**[05:25:05.790] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004322)  
And even kahit po yung ating statistic na yung ratio ng student to guidance counselors,

**[05:25:13.110] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004323)  
na 1 is to 400, dapat 1 is to 250,

**[05:25:16.530] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004324)  
hindi pa rin po ma-fulfill kahit yung 1 is to 250 na standard

**[05:25:22.230] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004325)  
that should be applicable.

**[05:25:24.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004326)  
na hindi na po kaya

**[05:25:26.080] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004327)  
ng current setup. So ang tanong ko po

**[05:25:28.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004328)  
Madam Chair, ano po ba

**[05:25:30.760] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004329)  
ang plans ng DOH

**[05:25:32.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-004330)  
para po matulungan na

**[06:10:05.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005605)

**[06:10:07.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005606)

**[06:10:10.620] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005607)

**[06:10:15.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005608)

**[06:10:20.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005609)

**[06:10:33.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005614)

**[06:10:43.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005617)

**[06:10:47.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005618)

**[06:10:49.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005619)

**[06:10:53.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005620)

**[06:11:00.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005621)

**[06:11:01.860] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005622)

**[06:11:03.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005623)

**[06:11:06.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005624)  
Para mas makapag-negotiate tayo at makapag-engage po ng mga doktor.

**[06:11:11.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005625)

**[06:11:16.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005626)

**[06:11:24.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005628)

**[06:11:27.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005629)

**[06:11:33.910] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005630)

**[06:11:39.670] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005631)

**[06:16:40.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005708)  
Preemptively, nagbaba na po kami sa mga region at may bago po kaming polisiyang nilabas na kami na po mismo ang pupunta doon sa mga komunidad na nalubog.

**[06:16:51.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005709)

**[06:16:57.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005710)

**[06:17:04.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005711)  
At saka yung dosaging po, iba't iba po yan.

**[06:17:07.060] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005712)

**[06:17:12.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005713)  
Kung ito po ay moderate risk or severe risk, iba po ang dosaging.

**[06:17:16.980] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005714)

**[06:17:21.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005715)  
So yung barangay health worker po ang kaakibat na kasama natin para mga lugat ng mga barangay.

**[06:17:28.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-005716)  
Tapos kami na po ang talagang pupunta para po mag-distribute ng gamot para naaayon po doon sa dapat na dosaging.

**[07:24:54.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007384)

**[07:25:17.880] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007385)

**[07:27:36.350] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-007409)

**[07:27:39.850] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-007410)

**[07:28:33.720] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007423)

**[07:28:48.420] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007426)

**[07:28:57.440] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007427)

**[07:29:04.960] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007428)

**[07:29:14.560] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007429)

**[07:29:18.900] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007430)  
Kaya hirap na hirap po sila talaga.

**[07:29:21.580] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007431)

**[07:29:26.400] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007432)

**[07:29:27.440] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007433)

**[07:29:32.280] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007434)
