**[07:29:46.440] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007435)  
Nandito po si Dr. June Aventura.

**[07:29:53.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007436)  
Maari pong paliwanagan dahil may mga financial considerations din po daw sila.

**[07:30:00.000] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-007437)  
Go ahead po, you're recognized.

**[07:30:05.860] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007438)  
Your Honor, yung pong 50% down payment huyan, yun sa pay patient po namin, pagka-schedule siya for elective surgery.

**[07:30:18.200] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007439)  
Because back in the teens, 2015, before that, meron siyang nalulugi yung heart center noon.

**[07:30:27.540] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007440)  
Then we have to pay yung mga pasyente na QFS for two years.

**[07:30:34.520] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007441)  
Tapos naubos yung pera namin for iipon.

**[07:30:39.000] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007442)  
So, ginawa namin, nakapag-down kasi hindi na nag-mabayad eh.

**[07:30:44.080] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007443)  
So, nagda-down na lang, sorry, nagda-down na lang para at least mabayaran yung hospital.

**[07:30:53.800] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007444)  
Hindi po, ganun eh. Kasi halos lahat ng mga humihingi sa akin ng GL, nag-iipon.

**[07:30:59.020] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007445)  
Nag-iipon sila ng 50% para may schedule sila na maoperahan.

**[07:31:03.940] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007446)  
Actually, tatlo na po yung pasyente na nabigyan ko po ng GL pero nakamataya na nila kasi hindi nila maipon yung 50%.

**[07:31:14.720] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007447)  
Eh meron naman po, lalo na po yung open heart surgery, meron naman po binibigay ang PhilHealth na 1 million yata o 900,000.

**[07:31:26.420] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007448)  
Eh bakit hindi na lang yun?

**[07:31:29.020] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007449)  
Kung i-down payment nila o kaya may mga buffer naman po tayo para yan na lang po ang pagsagot kung hindi makabayad ang pasyente.

**[07:31:37.540] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007450)  
Mga private patients po yung 50% down payment.

**[07:31:43.040] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007451)  
Anong private na sinasabi po ninyo?

**[07:31:45.440] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007452)  
Kasi may charity ho at saka may private. Yung private patients namin yung pinarequire namin ng 50% down payment.

**[07:31:52.800] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007453)  
Hindi naman po siguro hinihingi ng GL yung makaya ho ng private.

**[07:31:59.020] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007454)  
Yung mahihirap po, humihingi sila ng GL, nag-iipon sila ng GL para may 50% sila para ma-schedule silang ma-operahan.

**[07:32:08.880] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007455)  
Sana po tanggalin na lang po natin yan lalo na po sa mahihirap na pasyente para naman po magaan po ang buhay nila, di po ba?

**[07:32:18.420] Dr. June Aventura** (asr-007456)  
Pagka mahihirap po, charity po ang pinapasok namin.

**[07:32:22.240] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007457)  
Kung charity naman po kasi abutin sila ng siyam-siyam.

**[07:32:26.580] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007458)  
Pipipila sila ng madaling araw hanggang hati.

**[07:32:29.020] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007459)  
Pagting gabi para lang ano, kung hindi sila mabigyan itong araw na ito, pipila na naman sila sa isang araw.

**[07:32:36.420] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007460)  
Sana po magkaroon ng sistema na kung 100 lang po ang kaya ninyong ano yung na pasyente, di, bigyan na ng number yung 100.

**[07:32:44.700] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007461)  
Yung iba umuwi, bigyan na ng number para kinabukasan.

**[07:32:48.180] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007462)  
Ganun po, hirap na hirap po mga pasyente.

**[07:32:51.500] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007463)  
Sana naman po maawa kayo, mga government hospitals.

**[07:32:56.420] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007464)  
Yan lang po ang inaano ko.

**[07:32:59.020] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007465)  
Alas po talaga.

**[07:33:00.240] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007466)  
Yan po ang naano ko sa mga tao na reklamo nila na yun nga, yung 50% na yan na down.

**[07:33:08.460] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007467)  
Ewan ko po kung bakit naman.

**[07:33:10.260] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007468)  
Sana sa mahihirap.

**[07:33:11.760] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007469)  
Sabihin nyo, charity.

**[07:33:12.900] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007470)  
Pero hirap na hirap naman po silang kumuha ng charity.

**[07:33:16.180] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007471)  
Kasi nahihirapan din po silang pumila.

**[07:33:18.980] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007472)  
Sana po, ano na lang, alisin na lang po ninyo yan para naman magaan po ang buhay ng mga tao.

**[07:33:25.140] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007473)  
At saka, isa pa po.

**[07:33:27.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007474)  
Yung ano, sana po, magawa natin ng paraan yung zero balance policy na programa ng ating Pangulo.

**[07:33:36.520] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007475)  
Napakaganda pong programa yan.

**[07:33:38.920] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007476)

**[07:33:41.380] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007477)

**[07:33:45.240] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007478)

**[07:33:52.140] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007479)  
para po maka-avail yung mga mahihirap ng zero balance policy.

**[07:33:56.920] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007480)  
No po?

**[07:33:57.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007481)  
Opo, Your Honor.

**[07:33:58.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007482)  
Actually, nag-uusap na ako rin ng Philly Health at saka ang Heart Center

**[07:34:02.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007483)  
regarding sa pag-ayos ng mga urgent and emergent cases.

**[07:34:08.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007484)  
Kasi sila yung medyo tumatagal sa aming hospital kasi walang pambayad.

**[07:34:14.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007485)  
So hindi sila nakaka-uwi, hinihingi ng GL. Tapos walang down payment rin.

**[07:34:19.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007486)

**[07:34:25.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007487)  
So,

**[07:34:27.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007488)  
yung ano naman, yung ating 50%, ginagawa lang naman natin yun para mabayaran yung hospital at hindi maubos yung pondo.

**[07:34:37.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007489)

**[07:34:42.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007490)

**[07:34:44.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007491)  
Pagka emergent po, sorry po. Pagka emergent po, diretsyo yun, magagawa ka agad.

**[07:34:51.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007492)  
Pero pagka elective, yun ang nag-ano.

**[07:34:55.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007493)  
Sana po, ganun nga po ang mangyayari.

**[07:34:58.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007494)

**[07:35:03.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007495)

**[07:35:07.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007496)

**[07:35:13.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007498)

**[07:35:23.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007499)  
Kasi parang yatang may unobligated allotment pa kayo na 121 billion na hindi pa nagagamit.

**[07:35:33.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007500)  
Eh wala po tayong gamot sa mga hospitals. Ano po? Wala tayong mga gamot sa hospitals.

**[07:35:39.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007501)  
Kasi siguro nalilate po yung procurement o kaya hindi po nadideliver yung mga gamot, kaya na-expire.

**[07:35:47.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007502)

**[07:35:52.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007503)  
Para sa procurement po. Sana siguro mas maganda po kung mas maaga kayo mag-procure

**[07:35:58.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007504)  
para po yung mga ano, isama na po ninyo siguro sa presyo ninyo yung transportation.

**[07:36:07.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007505)  
Kasi baka doon nagkakaproblema sa logistic na hindi po nadideliver yung mga gamot sa mga hospitals sa mga probinsya.

**[07:36:19.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007506)  
Dahil wala tayong, wala kayo siguro.

**[07:36:22.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007507)  
Hindi siguro for transportation, hindi siguro naisama doon sa presyo ninyo o sa ano.

**[07:36:30.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007508)  
Ano bang nag-problema po natin sa procurement?

**[07:36:33.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007509)  
Kasi wala pong gamot ang mga hospitals eh. Ano bang problema natin doon?

**[07:36:40.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007510)

**[07:36:46.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007511)  
Okay.

**[07:36:47.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007512)

**[07:36:50.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007513)

**[07:36:51.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007514)

**[07:36:58.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007515)

**[07:37:04.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007516)  
Pero yung hospital po mismo, mga DOH hospitals po, ang procurement po ay devolved.

**[07:37:12.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007517)

**[07:37:16.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007518)

**[07:37:19.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007519)

**[07:37:27.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007520)  
Pero ma-monitor po natin yung stock out po nitong mga DOH hospitals din. At yun nga po yung efficiencies na na-mention ko kanina.

**[07:37:46.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007522)  
Kasi bumibili sa labas ang mga pasyente.

**[07:37:48.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007523)

**[07:37:53.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007524)

**[07:38:44.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007535)

**[07:38:53.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007538)

**[07:39:15.780] Rep. Milagros "Mila" Aquino-Magsaysay** (asr-007543)

**[07:40:09.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007551)  
Streamline po natin kasi po by law talagang hiwalay po yung AIT-STAC.

**[07:40:10.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007552)

**[07:40:12.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007554)

**[07:53:58.880] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007846)

**[07:54:35.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-007855)

**[07:54:40.380] PhilHealth President and CEO Dr. Beverly Lorraine Ho** (asr-007856)

**[07:54:48.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007858)

**[07:54:52.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007859)  
yan po yung legacy ninyo.

**[07:54:54.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007860)  
Kasi sa Lalawigan po, sa probinsya, yan po talaga dayin ng mga tao eh.

**[07:54:58.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007861)  
NGL.

**[07:54:59.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007862)

**[07:55:03.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007863)

**[07:55:05.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007864)

**[07:55:08.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007865)

**[07:55:10.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007866)

**[07:55:13.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007867)  
Mental health is a community-based approach.

**[07:55:17.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007868)

**[07:55:19.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007869)  
Kasi last week lang po, meron nung dalawang, actually in the last two weeks,

**[07:55:24.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007870)

**[07:55:34.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007871)  
And I know that the approach is community-based.

**[07:55:37.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007872)  
You know, in other words, imparting to non-licensed individuals

**[07:55:45.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007873)  
aspects of mental health care that can be imparted.

**[07:55:50.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007874)  
What is the status of that?

**[07:55:52.380] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-007875)  
And how do you monitor that that's being done?

**[07:55:58.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007876)  
Madam Chair, ang una nga po diyan, dahil po may kakulangan talaga po ng mga mental health professionals.

**[07:56:05.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007877)  
So, ang atin pong gagawin ay mag-deploy o mag-deputize ng iba pa pong mga training centers

**[07:56:12.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007878)  
para po mag-certify ng mental health professionals.

**[07:56:15.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007879)  
Pangalwa po ay paigtingin po yung peer-to-peer program dahil po,

**[07:56:24.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007880)  
karamihan po talaga mas nakikinig kapag ka-peer, ka-ano niya, ka-edad.

**[07:56:34.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007881)  
Ang payo nga po sa amin nung ako ay nasa med school,

**[07:56:38.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007882)  
pag nakonbinsi mo isang nagtangka at nag-verbalize na sila ay mag-suicide,

**[07:56:44.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007883)  
at nakuha mo na verbally din sabihin huwag mong ituloy, malaki po ang chance na hindi tutuloy.

**[07:56:50.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007884)  
So, yung peer-to-peer po is very important.

**[07:56:53.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007885)  
Pangatlo na po yung doon sa ating contact center na 24x7.

**[07:56:58.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007886)  
Yun lang medyo kailangan dagdagan siguro yung mga available share natin.

**[07:57:07.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007887)  
So, yung suicide surveillance po is very important.

**[07:57:11.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007888)  
Pag may mga nakikita na po,

**[07:57:13.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007889)  
yung kanilang mga peer na medyo meron pong mga actuation o nab-verbalize,

**[07:57:20.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007890)  
yun po yung pinaka-signalis po na baka magtuloy.

**[07:57:25.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007891)  
Kaya yun po yung ating setting-based impanelment ng mental health ay sa school-based nga po,

**[07:57:32.380] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007892)  
na i-align natin with YACAP.

**[07:57:35.380] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-007893)

**[07:57:47.540] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-007895)

**[07:57:54.540] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-007897)

**[07:58:02.540] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-007898)

**[07:58:12.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007900)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Good afternoon to DOH family.

**[07:58:16.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007901)  
Good afternoon po. Madam Chair, katangungan ko lang po sa nutrition program ng ating DOH.

**[07:58:29.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007902)  
Magkano po kaya yung continuing yung mga budget po natin for the stunting and wasting?

**[07:58:37.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007903)  
And then, ano po yung appropriate na-allocate for 2026?

**[07:58:43.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007904)  
And then, ano po yung how much is the budget for 2027?

**[07:58:49.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007905)  
So, sa atin po, can we give us the exact number of children currently stunted at saka wasted for 2025-2026?

**[07:59:00.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007906)  
And then, how much po ang binibigay natin ka-perahan sa each beneficiary for this program?

**[07:59:10.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007907)  
I know that the DOH, sinesentro po natin ang programa para sa mga children 0 to 59 months.

**[07:59:18.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007908)  
And of course, for our nutrition program, we have also the pregnant women and then the lactated mothers.

**[07:59:24.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007909)  
So, ano po yung ginagawa po nating programa sa ngayon?

**[07:59:28.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007910)  
And sapat ho ba ang pondo po natin for ano po yung natitira for 2026 and sa 2027?

**[07:59:38.540] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007911)  
Na naka-allocate sa NEP po natin, okay na ba po ba yan? Salamat po.

**[07:59:45.540] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007912)  
Thank you po. Madam Chair, ang ating prevalence of stunting noong 2025 ay 25.3%.

**[07:59:57.640] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007913)  
Ngayon po...

**[08:00:00.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007914)  
2026 dahil ongoing pa rin po. Ang target po namin ay maibaba ng 20.8 pero hintayin pa po natin until end of year kung ano po yung accomplishment rate natin.

**[08:00:10.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007915)  
At for 2027 po ay 19.4% po ang ating target na reduction ng stunting.

**[08:00:16.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007916)  
Ang ating budget for nutrition in the 2025 ga is 976 million. Yung nga lang po bumaba tayo ng 2026 ng 223 million at ngayon din po 2027 ay may trend po ng pababa ng another 209 million.

**[08:00:40.080] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007917)  
Pero ang ating sinestrengthen ay yung first 1000 day program po together with DSW.

**[08:00:46.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007918)  
With PhilHealth. Kasi kasama na po dito yung dun pa lang sa prenatal na mamonitor na natin kasi nga po yung MCP natin kasama na yung 14, 14 nga ba?

**[08:00:58.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007919)  
Ang prenatal. Yun pong 8 prenatal visits na kasama po yung monitoring ng growth rate po ng ating intrauterine yung ating bata.

**[08:01:14.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007920)  
At pagkatapos.

**[08:01:16.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007921)  
At pagkatapos nga po ay after delivery which is still covered substantially by PhilHealth for postnatal for postpartum.

**[08:01:26.120] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007922)  
And maganda po yung mungkahi kanina ni Governor nung itahi din pati po yung family planning at saka lactation during the, bakit kasi yung for postnatal parang isang buwan at kalahati lang po eh parang maigse.

**[08:01:43.600] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007923)  
So yun dun po ay kakabalik.

**[08:01:46.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007924)  
Sabit natin yung monitoring ng first 1000 day.

**[08:01:50.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007925)  
Pati yung lactating mother pa paano po yung ating tulong na gagawin.

**[08:01:54.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007926)  
So yun pong sa first 1000 day naman sa PhilHealth ay meron pong mga nutritional program na kasama yung RUTF at saka yung mga high protein diets na kinukover naman po ng ating PhilHealth program.

**[08:02:13.480] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007927)  
Thank you.

**[08:02:13.980] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007928)  
Thank you Madam Chair.

**[08:02:15.040] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007929)

**[08:02:43.780] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007934)

**[08:02:53.440] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007937)

**[08:03:11.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007938)  
So meron po tayong 747 million na nakalaan po sa anti-rabies vaccine and immunoglobulin.

**[08:03:40.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007945)

**[08:03:50.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007947)

**[08:03:55.510] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007948)  
So meron pa rin po talagang hindi natin na kukover.

**[08:03:59.450] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007949)  
Meron naman po tayong animal bite center ng PhilHealth na siya pong nagkukover.

**[08:04:05.350] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007950)  
Yun lang po.

**[08:04:05.970] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007951)  
Ang covered ng PhilHealth ay only Category 3.

**[08:04:09.750] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007952)  
Hindi pa po covered yung Category 2.

**[08:04:11.890] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007953)  
Pero ang public health intervention pong aming minumungkahi at mas tingin po namin mas cost effective ay bakunahan po yung ating pet.

**[08:04:20.910] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007954)  
Na yun po ay dapat din ma-isama sa budget po ng Department of Agriculture at saka yung iba pong local government units.

**[08:04:30.690] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007955)  
Kasi mas gagastos po tayo at tumataas po.

**[08:04:35.450] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007956)  
Nasa top three utilization po ng PhilHealth ang animal bite.

**[08:04:41.330] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007957)

**[08:04:44.250] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007958)  
Pero dahil nga po nagkulang yung ating budget, medyo hindi po namin maipag-uusap.

**[08:04:50.390] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007959)  
May roll out yung coverage ng Category 2 ng PhilHealth.

**[08:04:54.010] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-007960)  
So ang ano po namin talagang yung mga animal at saka yung pets po ang mabakunahan.

**[08:05:00.190] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007961)  
So Madam Chair, siguro doon sa DA para yung preventive measure, mas malaki kasi yung gasto sa gobyerno pag bumibili talaga tayo ng bakuna para sa mga, eto mga rabies sa mga ano natin, vaccines.

**[08:05:13.170] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007962)

**[08:05:19.230] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007963)

**[08:05:25.790] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007964)

**[08:05:28.870] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007965)

**[08:05:47.610] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007966)  
So it will really affect.

**[08:05:49.230] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007967)

**[08:05:54.690] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007968)

**[08:05:57.550] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007969)

**[08:06:10.610] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007972)

**[08:06:18.110] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007973)

**[08:06:19.230] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007974)

**[08:08:50.830] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-007998)

pero hindi po siya.

**[08:09:19.310] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008003)  
Wala pong...

**[08:09:20.830] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008004)

**[08:09:37.330] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008007)

sana yung tapos na na-infra, yun ang bibigyan natin ng equipments.

**[08:10:28.890] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008014)  
And then, sana po, kasi nanggaling po ako as a public health, nakita ko ho na yung mga pinupunduhan natin before,

**[08:10:37.670] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008015)  
yung mga BP up, yung mga weighing scale, kaya na po ng LGU na magpundo doon.

**[08:10:43.170] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008016)  
Sana ang punduhan ng DOH ay yung mga malalaking equipments like the...

**[08:10:50.830] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008017)  
Yung equipments na kailangan ho natin.

**[08:10:54.310] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008018)  
And then...

**[08:10:55.170] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008019)  
Thank you for caring.

**[08:10:55.930] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008020)  
Madam Chair, isa na lang.

**[08:10:57.230] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008021)  
Sa amin, yung DOH na...

**[08:11:00.230] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008022)  
Sa amin po, wala po kasing...

**[08:11:02.990] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008023)  
Wala po kami yung DOH Managed Hospital sa Kalinga Provincial Hospital until now, nasa Senate na po, and hope matatapos na rin to.

**[08:11:13.770] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008024)  
And sa mga FLR projects ho natin, na 2025-2026,

**[08:11:20.830] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008025)  
na FLR bago po yung napunduhan for the NEP on 2027 na Super RHU.

**[08:11:26.170] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008026)  
Salamat po, Madam Chair.

**[08:11:28.110] Rep. Caroline B. Agyao** (asr-008027)  
Salamat, Secretary and the DOH family.

**[08:11:31.270] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008028)  
Madam Chair, well noted po. Maganda po mga suggestions.

**[08:11:34.650] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008029)  
Thank you very much, Kong.

**[08:11:36.230] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008030)  
Ang next po natin is Kong Edwin Cruzado. Go ahead po, Kong.

**[08:11:40.610] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008031)  
Good evening to everyone.

**[08:11:44.310] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008032)  
To our chairman.

**[08:11:50.830] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008033)  
Sa ating Secretary of Health and Dr. Mercado, Dr. Ho.

**[08:11:57.710] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008034)  
Gusto kong pasalamatan ng PhilHealth kasi for the last two years, actually, you have already doubled the premium.

**[08:12:05.610] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008035)  
So maraming mga hospital na medyo nakaangat, nakabangon.

**[08:12:10.250] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008036)  
At saka yung mga doctors din, okay naman din ang natatanggap nila.

**[08:12:14.910] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008037)  
So thank you, Dr. Mercado. Ikaw yung ano, naging ano niyan.

**[08:12:20.830] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008038)  
Sa magandang trabaho mo, good job, no?

**[08:12:23.910] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008039)  
Sana ipagpatuloy ng ating Dr. Ho, na siyang president ngayon ng PhilHealth.

**[08:12:31.590] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008040)  
Actually, with regards to financing, medyo okay naman ang financing natin sa PhilHealth.

**[08:12:41.850] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008041)  
Yung utilization na lang ang dapat nating bantayan.

**[08:12:45.850] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008042)  
Saka ang challenge sa ating Secretary,

**[08:12:48.350] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008043)  
na sana wala nang,

**[08:12:50.830] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008044)  
masyadong expiring na gamot kasi

**[08:12:54.270] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008045)  
municipal health officer din ako for a long time

**[08:12:57.070] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008046)  
and a practitioner ng surgery.

**[08:13:00.390] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008047)  
And we always experience that na kung may mga expired na,

**[08:13:06.450] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008048)  
bibigyan na kami ng maraming supply.

**[08:13:09.730] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008049)  
So sana maging maingat tayo sa purchasing.

**[08:13:12.690] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008050)  
At saka dapat sana yung ating immunization program,

**[08:13:18.990] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008051)  
tutukan na natin para,

**[08:13:20.830] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008052)  
magkaroon na tayo ng mga supplies.

**[08:13:22.610] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008053)  
May mga question lang kasi ako, no?

**[08:13:25.550] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008054)  
Sa, with regards sa hospital,

**[08:13:28.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008055)  
kasi ang issue natin yung out of pocket is malaki, no?

**[08:13:34.470] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008056)  
Ang government hospital natin is 90% should be allocated for NBB.

**[08:13:43.510] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008057)  
Tama ba ko, Sec? No?

**[08:13:46.050] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008058)  
Okay.

**[08:13:46.950] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008059)  
And then sa private hospital,

**[08:13:49.590] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008060)  
should be 10% na hospital.

**[08:13:50.670] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008061)  
Ang bed capacity nila should be allocated for No-Balance Billing.

**[08:13:57.190] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008062)  
So, presently, ang reality is,

**[08:14:01.110] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008063)  
dahil kaya ng ating government hospital medical centers

**[08:14:05.450] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008064)  
to provide good equipments,

**[08:14:09.050] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008065)  
at saka pwedeng mag-private ang private practice doon,

**[08:14:13.230] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008066)  
karamihan ng ating mga pasyente ay nadadagsaan

**[08:14:16.230] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008067)  
doon sa mga medical centers.

**[08:14:18.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008068)  
Kasi we can afford good equipments sa ICU.

**[08:14:23.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008069)  
They can even afford to buy robotics in surgery.

**[08:14:28.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008070)  
Ang tanong ko lang dito is,

**[08:14:31.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008071)  
na-implement ba talaga yung 90% No-Balance Billing?

**[08:14:35.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008072)  
O baka mas marami ng private cases na kine-cater yung mga medical centers?

**[08:14:41.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008073)  
Wherein, the doctors, the consultants who are practicing there,

**[08:14:46.430] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008074)  
is charging,

**[08:14:47.870] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008075)  
hindi mababa, kundi mataas.

**[08:14:50.870] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008076)  
So, sana magkaroon tayo ng ceiling man lang doon sa singilan.

**[08:15:00.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008077)  
I appreciate, nung nagbisita ako doon sa Zamboanga Medical Center, sabi ko,

**[08:15:05.320] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008078)  
Doc Conti, paano mo ginagawa ang private dito sa hospital ninyo?

**[08:15:12.420] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008079)  
Ang sabi niya, he limited the bed capacity, the beds sa Zamboanga Medical Center, lima lang sa surgery.

**[08:15:21.040] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008080)  
Not more than that.

**[08:15:23.200] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008081)  
So I hope na pwede din natin gawain na ganun that there should be limits.

**[08:15:30.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008082)  
Nanon ko yung isang kaibigan ko, of course, galing din ako.

**[08:15:35.100] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008083)  
Meron daw silang 500 na waiting list sa private cases sa surgery.

**[08:15:42.740] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008084)  
So kung meron kayong 500 waiting list, so paano na lang yung mga indigents?

**[08:15:49.380] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008085)  
Wala na siguro silang lugar dyan.

**[08:15:51.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008086)  
So ang akin lang, there should be somebody who will monitor yung implementation.

**[08:15:58.280] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008087)  
And sana...

**[08:16:00.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008088)  
We will have reports also.

**[08:16:02.440] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008089)  
Kung ilan ang ginawang indigent cases, ilan ang naging private in every government national hospital.

**[08:16:13.260] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008090)  
Wala akong problema sa ating UHC, ha?

**[08:16:15.620] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008091)  
We are doing good, actually.

**[08:16:17.320] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008092)  
But yung implementation na lang natin, at saka yung mga doctors natin, sana mayroon naman silang gano'ng ano.

**[08:16:25.400] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008093)  
Yung awa, kasi walang pili, kahit na mahirap.

**[08:16:30.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008094)  
At sinisingil nila.

**[08:16:31.320] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008095)  
My worst experience is this.

**[08:16:34.320] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008096)  
Humihingi sa akin ng tulong isang pasyente.

**[08:16:38.760] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008097)  
Hemorrhidectomy.

**[08:16:39.700] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008098)  
Ginawa sa OPD.

**[08:16:41.380] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008099)  
Local anesthesia.

**[08:16:43.440] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008100)  
Ang hospital bill is 1,000.

**[08:16:45.620] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008101)  
But the doctor's fee is 60,000.

**[08:16:50.980] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008102)  
Humihingi ng tulong sa akin.

**[08:16:52.340] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008103)  
Sabi ko, nabayaran mo naman yan.

**[08:16:54.100] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008104)  
Di wala na tayong magawa.

**[08:16:56.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008105)  
I could not answer for the...

**[08:16:58.600] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008106)  
Hindi ko na ma-charge yan sa maip ko.

**[08:17:01.980] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008107)  
So...

**[08:17:02.300] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008108)  
Ano lang, monitoring lang po, sir.

**[08:17:05.500] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008109)  
And the second concern ko, yung problema ng hospital waste sa district.

**[08:17:12.300] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008110)  
Kasi marami tayong hospital waste.

**[08:17:14.860] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008111)  
Tapos, yung paano siya i-dispose.

**[08:17:21.740] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008112)  
So, we are paying 50,000 to 85,000 per kilo may kumukulikta.

**[08:17:26.980] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008113)  
Pwede bang matulungan din ang distrito na magkaroon ng...

**[08:17:30.300] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008114)  
Treatment, storage, and disposal facilities.

**[08:17:35.320] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008115)  
Yun lang po, SEC.

**[08:17:36.820] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008116)  
Thank you very much and magandang hapon sa inyong lahat.

**[08:17:40.300] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008117)  
Thank you, Kong Edwin.

**[08:17:42.900] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008118)  
Would SEC want to reply or sa mga issues ni Kong?

**[08:17:49.260] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008119)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[08:17:51.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008120)  
Yung PF nga po ay isang issue na kailangan po nating ayusin.

**[08:17:57.740] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008121)  
Ang plano po namin ay...

**[08:18:00.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008122)  
Ay talaga pong yung networking.

**[08:18:02.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008123)  
Pero ngayon, kulungan po na encounter yung...

**[08:18:06.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008124)  
Ang haba ng pila sa government hospital kasi ginagawang private.

**[08:18:10.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008125)  
Ang kalimitan po, mahaba ang pila sa government hospital kasi hinihintay nila yung charity ward beds.

**[08:18:16.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008126)  
Kaya yun po, nasusolusyonan na namin dito sa Manila.

**[08:18:20.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008127)  
Example po, ang PGH.

**[08:18:22.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008128)  
Meron po rin 300 na backlog sa breast.

**[08:18:25.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008129)  
Ang ginagawa po nila, may ginagawa yung work up sa PGH,

**[08:18:28.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008130)  
inoooperahan sa isang...

**[08:18:30.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008131)  
private hospital.

**[08:18:31.660] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008132)  
Pabanggiting ko na, sa Asian o sa Cardinal.

**[08:18:35.700] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008133)  
Ang PGH po yung magkiklaim ng aming Z-BEN.

**[08:18:39.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008134)  
Nag-agree na po sila with Cardinal, magkano po yung ibabayad doon sa operating portion.

**[08:18:44.800] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008135)  
Kaya yun po, nagagawa na po namin.

**[08:18:47.240] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008136)  
Kaya yun nga po yung ating tinatawag na network.

**[08:18:50.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008137)  
Kasi ang mga PhilHealth naman po is willing to pay.

**[08:18:54.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008138)  
And then we can add yung maipip doon sa mga outliers.

**[08:18:58.580] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008139)  
Pero po yung...

**[08:19:00.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008140)  
Dito po sa ginagawa po na, ginagawang private nung mga charity,

**[08:19:05.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008141)  
doon ng mga doktor po sa ating government hospital.

**[08:19:08.820] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008142)  
Pag-aaralan po natin paano po sila ma-engage.

**[08:19:13.000] Rep. Edwin Cruzado** (asr-008143)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[08:19:15.180] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008144)  
Thank you very much, Kong Edwin.

**[08:19:17.180] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008145)  
Secretary, thank you.

**[08:19:18.280] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008146)  
Ang next po is our Representative Christopher Yap.

**[08:19:23.700] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008147)  
Go ahead po, Kong.

**[08:19:28.330] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008148)  
Mayong hapon, kanatong tanan sa atong DOH family and their affiliate agencies.

**[08:19:34.850] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008149)  
So, establishment of the proposed hospital has been a long-standing legislative priority

**[08:19:44.270] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008150)  
that I have been actively pursuing for the past four years.

**[08:19:48.470] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008151)  
Beginning my first time as member of House of Representatives.

**[08:19:54.710] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008152)  
Sinabi dati ni Secretary Herbosa that na bawat probinsya na malayo,

**[08:19:59.750] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008153)  
kailangan mayroong DOH-run hospital.

**[08:20:03.050] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008154)  
It's been four years nung una kong sinabi dito,

**[08:20:06.670] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008155)  
about what I had personal experience.

**[08:20:11.450] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008156)  
More than a decade ago, my father died on the road.

**[08:20:16.350] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008157)  
Because of the nearest complete hospital is three hours away from us.

**[08:20:22.830] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008158)  
Walang ICU.

**[08:20:25.430] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008159)  
May sasakyan kami.

**[08:20:27.910] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008160)  
Paano pa kaya yung ordinary Filipino?

**[08:20:30.430] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008161)  
Na walang access, walang sasakyan.

**[08:20:34.390] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008162)  
So, I hope, Secretary-March.

**[08:20:38.240] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008163)  
I will consider it.

**[08:20:41.200] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008164)  
Gusto lang namin na may chance mabuhay yung mga tao, constituents namin sa Southern Leyta.

**[08:20:52.480] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008165)  
Madam Chair, again, dahil po gumaganda na yung payment ng PhilHealth,

**[08:21:00.940] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008166)  
gaya nga nang sabi ni Kong Gonzaga earlier,

**[08:21:05.760] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008167)  
may revenue stream po na pwede nang gamitin for expansion ng isang hospital.

**[08:21:11.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008168)  
So, it's just...

**[08:21:12.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008169)  
It's a matter talaga of may konting upfront investment.

**[08:21:16.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008170)  
Kasi pwede pong para gumanda po ang revenue stream ng isang district hospital,

**[08:21:21.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008171)  
kailangan talaga po mag-invest dun sa mga gamit.

**[08:21:24.780] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008172)  
So, yung initial phase, kasi matagal pa rin po kung sakaling ipapasay yung bill or legislation

**[08:21:31.280] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008173)  
ng conversion to a DOH hospital,

**[08:21:33.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008174)  
ay tulungan pong makapagpuntar ng mga basic equipment,

**[08:21:39.300] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008175)  
mga OR instruments,

**[08:21:42.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008176)  
at makapag-recruit po ng mga doktor,

**[08:21:44.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008177)  
pwede naman pong i-provide din yan ng DOH

**[08:21:47.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008178)  
kasi meron po tayong mga deployment ng ating mga fellows at saka mga residents.

**[08:21:54.040] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008179)  
Pwede pong yun muna yung near term na pwedeng gawin,

**[08:21:58.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008180)  
tapos po ay tulungan po dun sa HVEP dun sa mga gamit

**[08:22:02.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008181)  
habang hinihintay po natin yung pagpapasa ng legislation ng conversion.

**[08:22:10.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008182)  
So, yun po yung nakikita kong...

**[08:22:12.340] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008183)  
Dahil nga gagawa po tayo ng plano on a per-region basis,

**[08:22:16.720] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008184)  
ano po yung mga standard na dapat meron ng hospital na district, primary or secondary,

**[08:22:22.460] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008185)  
at ano po yung dapat meron sa provincial hospital,

**[08:22:25.020] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008186)  
at ano po yung nasa APEX hospital na usually regional hospital,

**[08:22:29.140] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008187)  
na nandun sa region na yun.

**[08:22:36.400] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008188)  
So, ngayon po sa kasalukuyan, ang pinakamalapit pong hospital is 3 hours away po.

**[08:22:41.260] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008189)  
Yung kumpletong hospital na may ICU po?

**[08:22:44.960] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008190)  
Di lang kumpleto.

**[08:22:45.820] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008191)  
Private vehicle pa yan po

**[08:22:48.700] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008192)  
na tatakbo pa sa 3 hours away

**[08:22:50.880] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008193)  
So, kung medyo

**[08:22:52.960] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008194)  
complicated cases

**[08:22:54.440] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008195)  
talagang hindi mabubuhay yung tao

**[08:22:56.320] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008196)  
So, I hope

**[08:22:58.300] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008197)  
tagal na naming

**[08:23:00.080] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008198)  
sinasabi dito

**[08:23:02.520] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008199)  
We had filed a bill

**[08:23:04.760] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008200)  
for the longest time

**[08:23:07.040] Rep. Christopherson "Coco" M. Yap** (asr-008201)  
We had our RDs here

**[08:46:20.680] Rep. Maria Angela S. Garcia** (asr-008574)

**[08:46:23.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008575)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[08:46:26.000] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008576)  
Yun nga po yung kailangan nating ipag-agree na set ng standards,

**[08:46:29.520] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008577)  
because the most efficient way is a spokes and hub arrangement,

**[08:46:33.840] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008578)  
where you will have catchment, then bring it to the next level, then bring it to the next level.

**[08:46:38.200] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008579)  
That's how a working health care provider network really looks like.

**[08:46:43.560] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008580)  
So, we will set standards to say these are the basic, and then these are the ones that need to be probably in per district,

**[08:46:51.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008581)  
one super health, for example.

**[08:46:53.420] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008582)  
And then for each province, one tertiary, and then for each region, one.

**[08:46:58.320] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008583)  
apex.

**[08:46:59.360] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008585)  
So, ganun po yung intent ng AGPN. Pero ngayon nga po dahil walang roadmap na fina-follow, ang dami pong naco-construct na hindi po according to the intent of the Universal Health Care healthcare provider referral network.

**[08:47:19.500] Secretary Edwin M. Mercado** (asr-008586)
